
ETHIOPIAN STARTUP PROCLAMATION NO. 1396/2025

Category 1: Designation & Eligibility
Articles 1–21 | Definitions, Eligibility, the Committee, Designation Lifecycle


Level 1  —  Easy ⭐
Easy ⭐  |  ቀላል

Q1  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q1  [Multiple Choice]  A business with no or limited operating history wants to qualify as a 'startup' under this proclamation. What else must it be doing to qualify?
A) It must be generating profit within its first year
B) It must be creating economic value through innovation
C) It must have at least five employees
D) It must be registered as a share company
አማ  ጥ1  ምንም ወይም ውስን የሥራ ታሪክ ያለው ንግድ ድርጅት በዚህ አዋጅ 'ስታርት አፕ' ሆኖ ለመመደብ ምን ማድረግ አለበት?
ሀ) በመጀመሪያ ዓመቱ ትርፍ ማሳየት አለበት
ለ) በፈጠራ የሚሰጥ ኢኮኖሚያዊ ዋጋ መፍጠር አለበት
ሐ) ቢያንስ አምስት ሠራተኞች ሊኖሩት ይገባል
መ) እንደ አክሲዮን ማኅበር ሆኖ መመዝገብ አለበት
EN  Answer: B) A startup is defined as creating economic value through innovation, with no or limited business history (Article 2/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ስታርት አፕ የሚተጋ ትርፍምዝገባ ወይም ውስን ዕድሜ ያለው ሲሆን፣ ዋናው መስፈርት በፈጠራ አማካኝነት ኢኮኖሚያዊ ዋጋ መፍጠር ነው (አዋጅ 2/1)

Q2  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q2  [True / False]  True or False: A startup must have a significant business history to qualify for designation under this proclamation.
አማ  ጥ2  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ ስታርት አፕ ለመሆን ጉልህ የሥራ ታሪክ ሊኖር ይገባል።
EN  Answer: False — a startup has no or limited business history; prior operating experience is not required.
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ስታርት አፕ ምንም ወይም ውስን የሥራ ታሪክ ያለው ድርጅት ነው። ቀደም ያለ ዕድሜ አያስፈልግም።

Q3  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q3  [Multiple Choice]  The Ministry of Innovation and Technology is referred to simply as 'the Ministry' throughout this proclamation. Why does this matter for a startup founder?
A) Because the Ministry is the only body that can revoke their designation
B) Because all references to 'the Ministry' — including for designation, grants, and directives — point to MoIT as the central authority
C) Because the Ministry of Finance has no role in the startup framework
D) Because the Ministry and the Council are the same institution
አማ  ጥ3  በዚህ አዋጅ 'ሚኒስቴር' ተብሎ የሚጠቀሰው ማን ነው? ለስታርት አፕ ባለቤት ይህ ምን አስፈላጊነት አለው?
ሀ) ሚኒስቴሩ ብቸኛ ምደባ ሊሰርዝ የሚችል አካል ስለሆነ
ለ) ምደባ፣ ድጎማ እና መመሪያ ጨምሮ ሁሉም ጉዳዮች ወደ ኢኢቴሚ (MoIT) ስለሚያቀኑ
ሐ) የፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴር ምንም ሚና ስለሌለው
መ) ሚኒስቴሩ እና ምክር ቤቱ አንድ አካል ስለሆኑ
EN  Answer: B) All references to 'the Ministry' point to the Ministry of Innovation and Technology as the central administrative authority for this proclamation
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) 'ሚኒስቴር' ሲባል ሁልጊዜ የኢኖቬሽን እና ቴክኖሎጂ ሚኒስቴር (ኢቴሚ) ሲሆን ዋናው አስተዳደራዊ አካል ነው

Q4  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q4  [Multiple Choice]  A startup creates an app that adapts existing mapping technology for local delivery businesses. Does this qualify as 'Tech-Enabled' under the proclamation?
A) No — Tech-Enabled requires creating entirely new technology from scratch
B) No — only hardware products qualify as tech-enabled
C) Yes — Tech-Enabled includes utilization, adoption, transplantation, or integration of existing technology
D) Yes — but only if the app has no foreign technology components
አማ  ጥ4  አንድ ስታርት አፕ ለአካባቢ ዴሊቨሪ ንግድ ያለውን ካርታ ቴክኖሎጂ ያስማሚ አፕ ፈጠረ። በዚህ አዋጅ ይህ 'ቴክ-ዳቤ' ነው?
ሀ) አይደለም — ቴክ-ዳቤ ከዜሮ አዲስ ቴክኖሎጂ መፍጠርን ብቻ ያካትታል
ለ) አይደለም — ሃርድዌር ምርቶች ብቻ ቴክ-ዳቤ ናቸው
ሐ) አዎ — ቴክ-ዳቤ ቀደም ያለ ቴክኖሎጂን መጠቀም፣ ማዳበር፣ ማስተሳሰር ወይም ማዋሃድ ያካትታል
መ) አዎ — ምንም የውጭ አካል ቴክኖሎጂ ከሌለ ብቻ
EN  Answer: C) Tech-Enabled includes utilization, adoption, transplantation, or integration of existing technology — not just creating new technology (Article 2/21)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ቴክ-ዳቤ ቀደም ያለ ቴክኖሎጂን መጠቀም፣ ማዳበር፣ ማስተሳሰር ወይም ማዋሃድን ያካትታል — አዲስ ቴክኖሎጂ ብቻ ማፍለቅ አይጠይቅም (አዋጅ 2/21)

Q5  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q5  [True / False]  True or False: Obtaining a Startup Designation is mandatory for all startups in Ethiopia.
አማ  ጥ5  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ የስታርት አፕ ምደባ ማግኘት በኢትዮጵያ ለሁሉም ስታርት አፖች ግዴታ ነው።
EN  Answer: False — designation is voluntary, but it is required to access any incentives and privileges under this proclamation.
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ምደባ በፈቃደኝነት ላይ የተመሠረተ ነው፤ ሆኖም ማንኛውንም ማበረታቻና ጥቅም ለማግኘት የግዴታ ቅድመ ሁኔታ ነው።


Level 2  —  Easy ⭐
Easy ⭐  |  ቀላል

Q6  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q6  [Multiple Choice]  An organization runs a 3-month intensive program helping early-stage businesses develop and launch. What type of startup ecosystem actor is this?
A) Incubator
B) Accelerator
C) Venture Capital
D) Angel Investor
አማ  ጥ6  አንድ ድርጅት ለጀማሪ ንግዶች 3 ወር የሚፈጅ ጥልቅ ፕሮግራም ያካሂዳል። ምን ዓይነት ኢኮሲስተም ተዋናይ ነው?
ሀ) ኢንኩቤተር
ለ) አክሰለሬተር
ሐ) ቬንቸር ካፒታል
መ) አንጀል ኢንቨስተር
EN  Answer: B) Accelerator — defined as a person or program providing intensive, time-bound support for startup establishment, growth, and development (Article 2/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) አክሰለሬተር — ለስታርት አፖች ምስረታ፣ ዕድገት እና ልማት ጥልቅ ጊዜ-ተወሰን ድጋፍ የሚሰጥ ሰው ወይም ፕሮግራም ነው (አዋጅ 2/2)

Q7  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q7  [True / False]  True or False: A designated startup can use grant funds to repay personal debts of its founders.
አማ  ጥ7  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ ተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ የሚቀበለውን ድጎማ ለባለቤቶቹ ዕዳ ለመክፈል መጠቀም ይችላል።
EN  Answer: False — repaying personal or corporate debts is explicitly prohibited as a use of grants.
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ግላዊ ወይም ኮርፖሬት ዕዳ መክፈያ ለድጎማ ዓላማ ፈጽሞ የተከለከለ ነው።

Q8  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q8  [Multiple Choice]  An organization provides shared office space, mentorship, and resources for early-stage startups on an ongoing basis without a fixed program deadline. What is it?
A) Accelerator
B) Venture Capital fund
C) Incubator
D) Co-working space only
አማ  ጥ8  አንድ ድርጅት ለጀማሪ ስታርት አፖች የጋራ ቢሮ ቦታ፣ አሰልጣኞች እና ሀብቶችን ያለ ቋሚ ፕሮግራም ጊዜ ይሰጣል። ምን ዓይነት ተዋናይ ነው?
ሀ) አክሰለሬተር
ለ) ቬንቸር ካፒታል
ሐ) ኢንኩቤተር
መ) የጋራ ሥራ ቦታ ብቻ
EN  Answer: C) Incubator — defined as a person that provides a supportive environment and resources for startups to foster their growth and development (Article 2/8)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ኢንኩቤተር — ለስታርት አፖች ዕድገት ተስማሚ ሁኔታ እና ሀብቶችን የሚሰጥ ድርጅት ነው (አዋጅ 2/8)

Q9  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q9  [Multiple Choice]  A startup wants to grow from 500 users to 500,000 without tripling its costs. Which characteristic of a startup does this describe?
A) Innovation
B) Tech-Enabled capability
C) Scalability
D) Economic value creation
አማ  ጥ9  አንድ ስታርት አፕ ወጪን ሦስት እጥፍ ሳያሻቅብ ተጠቃሚዎቹን ከ500 ወደ 500,000 ለማሳደግ ፈልጓል። ይህ ምን ባህሪ ያሳያል?
ሀ) ፈጠራ
ለ) ቴክ-ዳቤ ችሎታ
ሐ) ስኬሊቢሊቲ (አሳፈ)
መ) ኢኮኖሚያዊ ዋጋ ፈጠራ
EN  Answer: C) Scalability — the ability to increase market share at low cost by adapting quality, efficiency, and performance to withstand market demand (Article 2/17)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ስኬሊቢሊቲ — ዋጋ፣ ቅልጥፍና እና አፈጻጸምን ከገበያ ፍላጎት ጋር አጣጥሞ ዝቅ ባለ ዋጋ የገበያ ድርሻ ማሳደግ ችሎታ (አዋጅ 2/17)

Q10  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q10  [True / False]  True or False: A Tech-Enabled startup must always create brand-new technology that has never existed before.
አማ  ጥ10  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ ቴክ-ዳቤ ስታርት አፕ ፈጽሞ አዲስ የሆነ ቴክኖሎጂ መፍጠርን ብቻ ይጠይቃል።
EN  Answer: False — Tech-Enabled also includes utilization, adoption, transplantation, or integration of existing technology.
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ቴክ-ዳቤ ቀደም ያለ ቴክኖሎጂን መጠቀም፣ ማዳበር፣ ማስተሳሰር ወይም ማዋሃድን ያካትታል።


Level 3  —  Easy ⭐
Easy ⭐  |  ቀላል

Q11  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q11  [Multiple Choice]  The 'Council' in this proclamation oversees the startup ecosystem at the highest level. Which existing body serves as this Council?
A) The Council of Ministers
B) The National Digital Economy Council established under the Electronic Transaction Proclamation
C) The Ethiopian Investment Board
D) The National Startup Designation Committee
አማ  ጥ11  ይህ አዋጅ 'ምክር ቤቱ' ብሎ የሚጠቅሰው አካል ምን ነው?
ሀ) የሚኒስትሮች ምክር ቤት
ለ) በኤሌክትሮኒክ ግብይት አዋጅ ሥር የተቋቋመው ብሔራዊ ዲጂታል ኢኮኖሚ ምክር ቤት
ሐ) የኢትዮጵያ ኢንቨስትመንት ቦርድ
መ) ብሔራዊ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ኮሚቴ
EN  Answer: B) The National Digital Economy Council, established by Electronic Transaction Proclamation No. 1205/2020 (Article 2/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) በኤሌክትሮኒክ ግብይት አዋጅ ቁጥር 1205/2020 አዋጅ 6 ሥር የተቋቋመው ብሔራዊ ዲጂታል ኢኮኖሚ ምክር ቤት (አዋጅ 2/3)

Q12  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q12  [Multiple Choice]  Job creation, increased efficiency, export diversification, and social welfare enhancement are examples of which factor considered when assessing startups?
A) Scalability Factors
B) Innovation Factors
C) Economic Factors
D) Tech-Enabled Factors
አማ  ጥ12  የሥራ ፈጠራ፣ ቅልጥፍና መጨመር፣ ወጪ ማስፋፋት እና ማህበራዊ ደህና ሁኔታ ማሻሻል ምን ዓይነት ሁኔታዎች ናቸው?
ሀ) ስኬሊቢሊቲ ሁኔታዎች
ለ) ፈጠራ ሁኔታዎች
ሐ) ኢኮኖሚያዊ ሁኔታዎች
መ) ቴክ-ዳቤ ሁኔታዎች
EN  Answer: C) Economic Factors — these include efficiency/productivity, job creation, export growth, innovation, and social welfare enhancement (Article 2/5)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ኢኮኖሚያዊ ሁኔታዎች — ቅልጥፍና/ምርታማነት፣ የሥራ ፈጠራ፣ ወጪ ማሳደግ፣ ፈጠራ እና ማህበራዊ ደህና ሁኔታ ማሻሻልን ያካትታሉ (አዋጅ 2/5)

Q13  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q13  [True / False]  True or False: A 'Foreign Investor' under this proclamation can only be an individual foreign national — not a company.
አማ  ጥ13  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ 'የውጭ ኢንቨስተር' በዚህ አዋጅ ሥር ግለሰብ የውጭ ዜጋ ብቻ ሊሆን ይችላል — ኩባንያ ሊሆን አይችልም።
EN  Answer: False — it includes foreign nationals, foreign-owned enterprises, enterprises incorporated outside Ethiopia, joint enterprises, and Ethiopians abroad who prefer foreign investor treatment.
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ውጭ ዜጎችን፣ ውጭ-ባለቤት ድርጅቶችን፣ ከኢትዮጵያ ውጭ የተቋቋሙ ድርጅቶችን፣ የጋራ ድርጅቶችን እና ውጭ ሀገር የሚኖሩ ኢትዮጵያዊያን ያካትታል።

C) At least 20%
D) At least 25%
አማ  ጥ14  ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ለማግኘት ባለቤቱ/ቷ ቢያንስ ምን ያህል ካፒታል መያዝ አለበት/ሃት?

ሀ) ካፒታል፣ ፈቃዶች እና የመንግሥት ትስስር
ለ) ሀብቶች፣ እውቀት እና ድጋፍ
ሐ) የግብር ቅናሽ፣ ቦታ እና ፈቃዶች

Q20  🌐 EN + አማ

Q23  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q23  [Multiple Choice]  A startup operates in a new market where there are no existing licensing rules. It wants to test its product under government oversight before a full launch. What mechanism enables this?

B) The Startup Grant Program
C) The Regulatory Sandbox
D) A memorandum of understanding with the relevant ministry
አማ  ጥ23  አንድ ስታርት አፕ ፈቃድ ሳያስፈልጋቸው ምርቱን ለመፈተን የሚፈቅድ ሥርዓት ፈልጓል። ምን ዘዴ ያስፈልጋዋል?
ሀ) ከንግድ ሚኒስቴር ጊዜያዊ ፈቃድ
ለ) ስታርት አፕ ድጎማ ፕሮግራም
ሐ) ቁጆ ሳጥን (Regulatory Sandbox)
መ) ከሚመለከተው ሚኒስቴር ጋር የሚደረግ ስምምነት
EN  Answer: C) The Regulatory Sandbox — allows testing of new products, services, or processes under the regulatory authority's control and oversight, prior to market introduction (Article 2/16)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ቁጆ ሳጥን — አዲስ ምርቶች፣ አገልግሎቶች ወይም ሂደቶች ወደ ገበያ ከመቅረባቸው በፊት በቁጆ ባለሥልጣን ቁጥጥር ሥር ለመፈተን ያስችላል (አዋጅ 2/16)

Q24  🌐 EN + አማ

B) Venture Capital

አማ  ጥ24  አክሲዮኖቹ በፈቃድ ያለ የሙያ ምንዛሬ ቦርሳ ላይ የሚሸጡ ኩባንያ ምን ዓይነት ድርጅት ነው?
ሀ) ስታርት አፕ ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ

መ) ተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ

አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ህዝባዊ ኩባንያ — አክሲዮኖቹ ብቁ ምዝገባ ወይም ያልተደራጀ ኦቨር-ዘ-ካውንተር ገበያ ላይ የሚሸጡ ናቸው (አዋጅ 2/15)

Q25  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q25  [True / False]  True or False: The Digital Startup Portal can only be used for designation applications and cannot serve other purposes under the proclamation.
አማ  ጥ25  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ ዲጂታል ስታርት አፕ ፖርታል ለምደባ ማመልከቻ ብቻ ጥቅም ላይ ሊውል ይችላል — ሌሎች ዓላማዎች አሉት ብሎ ሕጉ አይቀበልም።
EN  Answer: False — the Ministry may also use the portal to discharge other relevant functions under the proclamation beyond designation applications.
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ሚኒስቴሩ ሌሎች ተዛማጅ ተግባሮችን ለማከናወን ፖርታሉን ሊጠቀምበት ይችላል (አዋጅ 4/4)።


Level 6  —  Medium ⭐⭐
Medium ⭐⭐  |  መካከለኛ

Q26  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q26  [Multiple Answers]  Which of the following are functions of the Ministry under this proclamation? (Select ALL that apply)
A) Coordinate startup ecosystem development actors
B) Grant startup designations based on committee decisions
C) Manage the Startup Grant Program
D) Set minimum capital requirements for foreign investors
አማ  ጥ26  ከሚከተሉት ሚኒስቴሩ በዚህ አዋጅ ሥር ያሉት ተግባሮች የትኞቹ ናቸው? (ሁሉንም ምረጥ)
ሀ) የስታርት አፕ ኢኮሲስተም አሳፊ ልማት ተዋናዮችን ማቀናጀት
ለ) ምደባ ምስክር ወረቀቶችን ለኮሚቴ ውሳኔ መሠረት ማቅረብ
ሐ) ስታርት አፕ ድጎማ ፕሮግራምን ማስተዳደር
መ) ለውጭ ኢንቨስተሮች ዝቅተኛ ካፒታል መስፈርቶችን ማዘጋጀት
EN  Answer: A, B, and C — setting minimum capital requirements for foreign investors is not a Ministry function (Article 4/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ፣ ለ እና ሐ — ለውጭ ኢንቨስተሮች ዝቅተኛ ካፒታል መስፈርቶችን ማዘጋጀት የሚኒስቴሩ ተግባር አይደለም (አዋጅ 4/1)

Q27  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q27  [Sequence]  Arrange these steps in CORRECT order for a startup to obtain designation:
(1) Apply through the Digital Startup Portal
(2) Committee evaluates the application
(3) Committee submits results to the Ministry
(4) Ministry issues the designation certificate
አማ  ጥ27  ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ለማግኘት ትክክለኛው ቅደም ተከተል አስቀምጥ:
(1) ዲጂታል ስታርት አፕ ፖርታል በኩል ማመልከቻ ማቅረብ
(2) ኮሚቴ ማመልከቻ ይዳኛል
(3) ኮሚቴ ውጤቱን ለሚኒስቴሩ ያቀርባል
(4) ሚኒስቴሩ ምደባ ምስክር ወረቀት ይሰጣል
EN  Answer: Correct order: 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 (Articles 9 and 11)
አማ  መልስ፡ ትክክለኛ ቅደም ተከተል፡ 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 (አዋጆች 9 እና 11)

Q28  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q28  [Match]  Match each designation to its correct initial validity period:
• Startup Designation: _____
• Startup Ecosystem Builder Designation: _____
• Maximum total designation period for a startup: _____
አማ  ጥ28  እያንዳንዱን ምደባ ዓይነት ከትክክለኛ የጊዜ ገደቡ ጋር አዛምድ:
• ስታርት አፕ ምደባ: _____
• ስታርት አፕ ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ምደባ: _____
• ከፍተኛ ጠቅላላ ምደባ ጊዜ: _____
EN  Answer: Startup Designation = 2 years (Art. 13/1); Ecosystem Builder = 5 years (Art. 19/1); Maximum startup total = 8 years (Art. 13/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ = 2 ዓመት (አዋጅ 13/1); ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ = 5 ዓመት (አዋጅ 19/1); ከፍተኛ ጠቅላላ = 8 ዓመት (አዋጅ 13/4)

Q31  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q31  [Multiple Answers]  An unregistered individual wants to obtain startup designation. Which conditions must they meet? (Select ALL that apply)
A) Own or demonstrate ownership of the product, process, or service
B) Hold at least 25% of capital as founder
C) Not be a Public Company
D) Have been operating for at least 1 year
አማ  ጥ31  ያልተመዘገበ ግለሰብ ምደባ ለማግኘት ምን ሁኔታዎች ያስፈልጉታል? (ሁሉንም ምረጥ)
ሀ) ምርቱን ወይም አገልግሎቱን ባለቤትነቱ ወይም ትዕይ ማሳየት
ለ) ቢያንስ 25% ካፒታል ባለቤት ሆኖ
ሐ) ህዝባዊ ኩባንያ አለመሆን
መ) ቢያንስ 1 ዓመት ሲሠሩ መቆየት
EN  Answer: A, B, and C — no minimum operating period is required for unregistered individuals (Article 7/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ፣ ለ እና ሐ — ዝቅተኛ የሥራ ጊዜ አይጠቀስም (አዋጅ 7/2)

Q32  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q32  [Sequence]  Arrange in CORRECT order the suspension-to-reinstatement process for a designated startup:
(1) Ministry notifies startup of the cause and required corrective actions
(2) Startup rectifies shortcomings within 30 working days
(3) Suspension is lifted and the designation becomes valid again
(4) Ministry determines the startup has failed its obligations
አማ  ጥ32  የስታርት አፕ ምደባ ታኃዲሶ (suspension) ቅደም ተከተል አስቀምጥ:
(1) ሚኒስቴሩ ምክንያቱን እና የሚጠይቀውን ማስተካከያ ያሳውቃል
(2) ስታርት አፑ ጉድለቱን በ30 ቀናት ውስጥ ያስተካክላል
(3) ታኃዲሶ ተነስቶ ምደባ ይሠራል
(4) ሚኒስቴሩ ስታርት አፑ ግዴታውን ያልፈፀመ ስለሆነ ያስቀምጣል
EN  Answer: Correct order: 4 → 1 → 2 → 3 (Article 14)
አማ  መልስ፡ ትክክለኛ ቅደም ተከተል፡ 4 → 1 → 2 → 3 (አዋጅ 14)

Q33  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q33  [Match]  Match each obligation to its correct timeframe:
• Inform Ministry of structural changes: _____
• Rectify shortcomings after Ministry notification: _____
• Renewal application submission deadline: _____
አማ  ጥ33  እያንዳንዱን ግዴታ ከትክክለኛ ጊዜ ገደቡ ጋር አዛምድ:
• ሚኒስቴሩን ስለ ድርጅታዊ ለውጦች ማሳወቅ: _____
• ሚኒስቴሩ ካሳወቀ በኋላ ጉድለቶች ለማስተካከል: _____
• የምደባ ታደሰ ማመልከቻ ቀነ-ሰጠ: _____
EN  Answer: Inform Ministry of changes = within 10 working days (Art. 12/1/e); Rectify shortcomings = within 30 working days (Art. 12/3); Renewal application = 30 working days before designation expiry (Art. 13/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሚኒስቴሩን ማሳወቅ = ከ10 የሥራ ቀናት ውስጥ (አዋጅ 12/1/ሀ); ጉድለቶች ማስተካከያ = ከ30 የሥራ ቀናት ውስጥ (አዋጅ 12/3); ታደሰ ማመልከቻ = ከምደባ ቀን 30 የሥራ ቀናት ቀደም (አዋጅ 13/2)

Q34  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q34  [Multiple Answers]  Which of the following are grounds for SUSPENSION of a startup designation? (Select ALL that apply)
A) Failure to fulfil obligations under the proclamation
B) Failure to provide timely and accurate information to the Ministry
C) Failure to carry out the activities for which designation was obtained
D) Failure to submit a renewed business license
E) Voluntary termination of business activity
አማ  ጥ34  ለስታርት አፕ ምደባ ታኃዲሶ (suspension) ምክንያቶች የትኞቹ ናቸው? (ሁሉንም ምረጥ)
ሀ) ግዴታዎቹን አለ መፈፀም
ለ) ለሚኒስቴሩ ወቅታዊ እና ትክክለኛ መረጃ አለ ማቅረብ
ሐ) ምደባ ለተሰጠለት ዓላማ አለ ሠርቶ መቆየት
መ) ንግድ ፈቃዱን አለ ታደሰ
ሠ) ንግዱን በፈቃደኝነት ማቆም
EN  Answer: A, B, C, and D — voluntary termination leads to revocation, not suspension (Article 14/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ፣ ለ፣ ሐ እና መ — ንግዱን በፈቃደኝነት ማቆም ምደባ ስርጭት (revocation) ያስፈልጋል እንጂ ታኃዲሶ አይደለም (አዋጅ 14/1)

Q35  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q35  [Multiple Choice]  What is the main role of the National Startup Designation Committee?
A) Issue grants directly to startups
B) Receive and evaluate designation applications, then submit results to the Ministry
C) Approve the Startup Grant Program budget
D) Suspend startup designations unilaterally
አማ  ጥ35  የብሔራዊ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ኮሚቴ ዋና ሚና ምንድን ነው?
ሀ) ለስታርት አፖች ድጎማ ቀጥታ ማቅረብ
ለ) ምደባ ማመልከቻዎችን ተቀብሎ፣ ሰምሮ ውጤቱን ለሚኒስቴሩ ማቅረብ
ሐ) ስታርት አፕ ድጎማ ፕሮግራም ወጪ ማፅደቅ
መ) የምደባ ታኃዲሶ ብቻውን ማወጅ
EN  Answer: B) The Committee receives applications, evaluates their merits, and submits results to the Ministry (Article 11/1/a)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ኮሚቴው ማመልከቻዎቹን ተቀብሎ፣ ብቁነቱን ሰምሮ ውጤቱን ለሚኒስቴሩ ያቀርባል (አዋጅ 11/1/ሀ)

Q36  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q36  [Multiple Answers]  Which of the following can be designated as Startup Ecosystem Builders? (Select ALL that apply)

B) Accelerators
C) Non-governmental organizations
D) Research and development agencies
E) Individual startup founders
አማ  ጥ36  የስታርት አፕ ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ሆኖ ሊሰጠው የሚችለው ማን ነው? (ሁሉንም ምረጥ)
ሀ) ኢንኩቤተሮች
ለ) አክሰለሬተሮች
ሐ) መንግሥታዊ ያልሆኑ ድርጅቶች
መ) ምርምር እና ልማት ድርጅቶች
ሠ) ግለሰብ ስታርት አፕ ባለቤቶች

Q37  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q37  [Sequence]  Arrange in CORRECT order the revocation process when falsified documents are discovered:
(1) Ministry notifies the startup in writing to submit its objection
(2) Objection is reviewed; if unsatisfactory, revocation proceeds
(3) Designation is revoked
(4) Ministry discovers the designation was based on falsified documents
አማ  ጥ37  ሰነዶቹ ሐሰተኛ ሆነው ሲገኙ ምደባ ስርጭት ቅደም ተከተል አስቀምጥ:
(1) ሚኒስቴሩ ስታርት አፑን በጽሑፍ ያሳውቃዋል — ምልሃቱ ሊቀርብ ይችላል
(2) ምልሃቱ ተዳኘ — ካልበቃ ምደባ ስርጭት ይቀጥላል
(3) ምደባ ተሰረዘ
(4) ሚኒስቴሩ ምደባ ሐሰተኛ ሰነድ ላይ እንደ ተሰጠ ያስቀምጣል
EN  Answer: Correct order: 4 → 1 → 2 → 3 (Articles 15/1/c and 15/2-3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ትክክለኛ ቅደም ተከተል፡ 4 → 1 → 2 → 3 (አዋጆች 15/1/ሐ እና 15/2-3)

Q38  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q38  [Match]  Match the Council's oversight obligations:
• Frequency of stakeholder consultation: _____
• Who conducts the annual audit: _____
• When the audit report must be received: _____
አማ  ጥ38  የምክር ቤቱን ቁጥጥር ኃላፊነቶች አዛምድ:
• ዓመታዊ ኦዲት አሳካ: _____
• የባለድርሻ ምክክር ድግግሞሽ: _____
• ሪፖርት ሊቀርብ የሚገባበት ቀን: _____
EN  Answer: Stakeholder consultation = at least once a year (Art. 5/1/i); Annual audit = by external auditor licensed by the Accounting and Audit Board of Ethiopia (Art. 5/1/f); Audit report = within 3 months after end of fiscal year (Art. 5/1/g)
አማ  መልስ፡ ዓመታዊ ኦዲት = የኢትዮጵያ ሂሳብና ኦዲት ቦርድ ፈቃድ ያለው ሦስተኛ ወገን ኦዲተር (አዋጅ 5/1/ሸ); ምክክር = ቢያንስ ዓመት አንድ ጊዜ (አዋጅ 5/1/ጠ); ሪፖርት = የበጀት ዓመት ካለቀ ከ3 ወር ውስጥ (አዋጅ 5/1/ዘ)

Q39  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q39  [Multiple Choice]  What automatically triggers suspension of a startup designation without any precondition or Ministry decision?
A) Failure to submit periodic reports to the Ministry
B) Suspension of the startup's business license by a relevant government organ
C) A change in the startup's corporate structure
D) Failure to achieve its stated growth objectives
አማ  ጥ39  ሌላ መንግሥታዊ አካል ስታርት አፑ ንግድ ፈቃዱን ሲያቋርጥ ምደባው ምን ይሆናል?
ሀ) ምንም — ምደባ እና ፈቃድ ሙሉ ነፃ ናቸው
ለ) ምደባ ያለ ምንም ቅድሚያ ሁኔታ ራሱን በራሱ ይቋረጣል
ሐ) ስታርት አፑ ከ10 የሥራ ቀናት ውስጥ ሚኒስቴሩን ማሳወቅ አለበት
መ) ሚኒስቴሩ ሁኔታውን ተዳኝቶ ይወሰናል
EN  Answer: B) Suspension of the business license or competence certificate by a relevant government organ automatically suspends the designation (Article 14/5)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ተዛማጅ መንግሥታዊ አካል ፈቃዱን ሲያቋርጥ ምደባው ያለ ቅድሚያ ሁኔታ ራሱን በራሱ ይቋረጣል (አዋጅ 14/5)

Q41  🌐 EN + አማ

ሀ) የንግድ ምዝገባ፣ ፈቃድ ወይም የኢንቨስትመንት ፈቃድ

አማ  ጥ42  ዲጂታል ስታርት አፕ ፖርታሉ ሦስቱ ዋና ተግባሮቹን በቅደም ተከተል አስቀምጥ:

• Academic representation from: _____
• Committee chairperson: _____
አማ  ጥ43  ለብሔራዊ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ኮሚቴ አባልነት ምንጮቹን አዛምድ:

አማ  መልስ፡ መንግሥት = ኢቴሚ፣ የሠራተኛ ሚ.፣ ኢንዱስትሪ ሚ.፣ ትምህርት ሚ. እና ሌሎች (አዋጅ 10/3/ሀ); ትምህርት = ከፍተኛ ትምህርት ተቋማት (አዋጅ 10/3/ለ); ሊቀ-ሊቃውንት = ኢቴሚ (አዋጅ 10/3/ሠ)

Q44  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q44  [Multiple Answers]  Which of the following are grounds for REVOCATION of a startup ecosystem builder designation? (Select ALL that apply)
A) Voluntary termination of business activity
B) Designation issued based on a falsified document
C) Use of designation for an unauthorized purpose
D) Declared bankrupt by a court
E) Failure to renew the designation
አማ  ጥ44  ለስታርት አፕ ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ምደባ ስርጭት (revocation) ምክንያቶች የትኞቹ ናቸው? (ሁሉንም ምረጥ)
ሀ) ንግዱን በፈቃደኝነት ማቆም
ለ) ሐሰተኛ ሰነድ ላይ የተሰጠ ምደባ
ሐ) ምደባ ላልሆነ ዓላማ ሲጠቀሙ
መ) ፍርድ ቤት የኪሳራ ፍርድ ሲሰጥ
ሠ) ምደባ ሳይታደስ ሲቀር
EN  Answer: A, B, C, D, and E — all five are grounds for revocation (Article 21/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ፣ ለ፣ ሐ፣ መ እና ሠ — ሁሉም አምስት ምደባ ስርጭት ምክንያቶች ናቸው (አዋጅ 21/1)

Q45  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q45  [Multiple Choice]  A startup receives the Ministry's revocation notice. What happens if it does not submit any objection within 30 working days?
A) The designation is suspended for another 30 days
B) The designation is automatically revoked
C) The designation remains valid pending further review
D) The Ministry issues a final warning before proceeding
አማ  ጥ45  ስታርት አፕ ሚኒስቴሩ ካሳወቀ በ30 የሥራ ቀናት ውስጥ ምልሃቱን ካላቀረበ ምን ይሆናል?
ሀ) ምደባ ለተጨማሪ 30 ቀናት ታኃዲሶ ይሆናል
ለ) ምደባ ራሱን በራሱ ይሰረዛል
ሐ) ምደባ ተጨማሪ ምርምር ድረስ ትክክለኛ ሆኖ ይቆያል
መ) ሚኒስቴሩ የመጨረሻ ማስጠንቀቂያ ይሰጣል
EN  Answer: B) If no objection is submitted within 30 working days of receiving the notice, the designation shall be revoked (Article 15/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቁርጥ ቀን ካለፈ ሳያቀርብ ከቀረ ምደባ ይሰረዛል (አዋጅ 15/3)

• ስታርት አፕ ኢኮሲስተም ስትራቴጂ ዓላማ: _____

አማ  መልስ፡ ዓመታዊ ኦዲት = ሂሳብ ቦርድ ፈቃድ ያለው ኦዲተር (አዋጅ 5/1/ሸ); ስትራቴጂ = ለብሩህ ኢኮሲስተም ሀብቶችን ለማመቻቸት (አዋጅ 5/1/ሐ); ድጎማ = ግልጽነትና ተጠያቂነት ማረጋገጥ (አዋጅ 5/1/ደ)

Q49  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q49  [Multiple Choice]  A foreign national permanently residing abroad prefers treatment as a foreign investor. Her startup receives designation in Ethiopia. What is her startup's status?
A) It is treated as a domestic startup with all domestic incentives
B) It qualifies as a Foreign Startup under the proclamation's definition
C) It is ineligible for designation because the founder is abroad
D) Its status depends on how many Ethiopian employees it has
አማ  ጥ49  ውጭ ሀገር ቋሚ ነዋሪ የሆነ ኢትዮጵያዊ የውጭ ኢንቨስተር ሆኖ ሊስተናገድ ፈልጓል። ስታርት አፑ ምን ዓይነት ምደባ ያገኛል?
ሀ) ሁሉም ሀገር ውስጥ ማበረታቻዎች ያሉ ሀገር ውስጥ ስታርት አፕ
ለ) በዚህ አዋጅ ፍቺ ሥር የውጭ ስታርት አፕ
ሐ) ባለቤቱ ውጭ ስለሆነ ምደባ አይቻለውም
መ) ምደባው በኢትዮጵያ ሠራተኞቹ ቁጥር ይወሰናል
EN  Answer: B) An Ethiopian permanently abroad who prefers foreign investor treatment (Article 2/6/e) makes their startup a Foreign Startup (Article 2/7)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ውጭ ሀገር ቋሚ ነዋሪ ኢትዮጵያዊ የውጭ ኢንቨስተር ሆኖ ሊስተናገድ ከፈቀደ (አዋጅ 2/6/ሠ) ስታርት አፑ የውጭ ስታርት አፕ ይሆናል (አዋጅ 2/7)

Q50  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q50  [Multiple Choice]  Who appoints the members of the National Startup Designation Committee?
A) The Minister of Innovation and Technology
B) The Council
C) The Council of Ministers
D) The President of Ethiopia
አማ  ጥ50  ብሔራዊ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ኮሚቴ አባሎቹ የሚሾሙት ማን ነው?
ሀ) የኢኖቬሽን ሚኒስቴር ሚኒስትር
ለ) ምክር ቤቱ
ሐ) የሚኒስትሮች ምክር ቤት
መ) የኢትዮጵያ ፕሬዚዳንት
EN  Answer: B) The Council (Article 10/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ምክር ቤቱ (አዋጅ 10/2)


Level 11  —  Hard ⭐⭐⭐

Q51  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q51  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: A startup received designation 4 years ago. It recently completed a merger that fundamentally changed its structure, taking it outside the definition of a startup. What is the correct legal outcome?
A) The startup can apply for renewal as usual at expiry
B) The Ministry must revoke the designation
C) The designation is automatically suspended
D) The startup has 30 days to reverse the merger
አማ  ጥ51  ሁኔታ፡ ስታርት አፕ ከ4 ዓመት ቀደም ምደባ ቀቀ ወጣ። ቅርቡ ውህደቱ ፍቺውን ካፈረሰ፣ ምን ሕጋዊ ውጤት አለ?
ሀ) ስታርት አፑ ምደባ ሊታደስ ይችላል
ለ) ሚኒስቴሩ ምደባ መሰረዝ አለበት
ሐ) ምደባ ራሱን በራሱ ይቋረጣል
መ) ስታርት አፑ ወደ ቀደሙ ደረጃ ለመመለስ 30 ቀናት አለው
EN  Answer: B) A merger that takes a startup outside the definition and eligibility requirements is a ground for revocation (Article 15/1/b)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ፍቺውን ካፈረሰ ውህደት ምደባ ስርጭት ምክንያት ነው (አዋጅ 15/1/ለ)

Q52  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q52  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT about the relationship between startup designation and business operation?
A) A startup cannot legally operate without obtaining a designation
B) Designation is mandatory for all startups in Ethiopia
C) Designation is voluntary but is a prerequisite to access incentives and privileges under this proclamation
D) Designation is required only for foreign startups
አማ  ጥ52  ስታርት አፕ ምደባ እና ንግድ ሥራ ምን ትክክለኛ ግንኙነት አለው?
ሀ) ሳያሰጡ ሕጋዊ ሆኖ ሊሠራ አይችልም
ለ) ምደባ ለሁሉም ስታርት አፖች ግዴታ ነው
ሐ) ምደባ በፈቃደኝነት ሆኖ ማበረታቻ ለማግኘት ቅድሚያ ሁኔታ ነው
መ) ለውጭ ስታርት አፖች ብቻ ግዴታ ነው
EN  Answer: C) Designation is voluntary but is the prerequisite for any incentives (Articles 6/1 and 6/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ምደባ በፈቃደኝነት ሆኖ ማበረታቻ ለማግኘት የማይቀር ቅድሚያ ሁኔታ ነው (አዋጆች 6/1 እና 6/2)

Q53  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q53  [Exception Question]  EXCEPTION: Under what circumstance does the one-year restriction on reapplying for a grant NOT apply to a startup that previously received one?
A) If the startup has changed its business model significantly
B) If the startup was legally liquidated
C) If the startup secured new private investment
D) If the startup received designation under a different name
አማ  ጥ53  ልዩ ሁኔታ፡ ቀደም ያዳ ስታርት አፕ ሕጋዊ ፈሳሽ ሁኔታ ካለፈ የአንድ ዓመት ድጎማ መጠቀቀ ልክ ከቶ አልተፈፃም?
ሀ) ዕዳ ካዛ አዲስ ስታርት አፕ ካዛ ጀምሮ 2 ዓመት
ለ) ሕጋዊ ፈሳሽ ሁኔታ ከ25/3 እና 25/4 ልክ ያነሳዋል
ሐ) ስም ሲቀይሩ ብቻ ወሰኑ ይወድቃል
መ) ኮሚቴ ፈቀደ ቢባል ብቻ ሊተፈፀም ይችላል
EN  Answer: B) Legal liquidation removes the reapplication restriction (Article 25/5)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሕጋዊ ፈሳሽ ሁኔታ ከ25/3 እና 25/4 ልክ ያስወግዳዋል (አዋጅ 25/5)

Q54  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q54  [Application Question]  APPLICATION: A startup was suspended for failing to provide timely information. It rectifies the problem within 30 working days of notification. What is the correct outcome?
A) The startup must reapply for designation from scratch
B) The suspension is lifted and the designation becomes valid
C) The startup pays a reinstatement penalty
D) The Ministry reviews and decides within an additional 30 days
አማ  ጥ54  ትጋት፡ ስታርት አፕ ትክክለኛ መረጃ ባለ ለመስጠቱ ታኃዲሶ ደርሶበታል። ከ30 ቀን ሳያስተካክል ቢቆይ ምን ይሆናል?
ሀ) ምደባ ሰርዞ ከዜሮ ዳቤ ሊጀምር ይገባዋል
ለ) ታኃዲሶ ተነስቶ ምደባ ይሠራል
ሐ) ቀጫ ሰፍቶ ቅጣት ይክፈላል
መ) ሚኒስቴሩ ተጨማሪ 30 ቀናት ፈቃጅ ምርምር ያካሂዳል
EN  Answer: B) Where defects causing suspension are rectified within the prescribed time, the suspension shall be lifted (Article 14/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ታኃዲሶ ምክንያቶቹ ሲስተካከሉ ታኃዲሶ ተነስቶ ምደባ ትክክለኛ ይሆናል (አዋጅ 14/4)

Q55  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q55  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT CORRECTLY distinguishes between suspension and revocation of a startup designation?
A) Suspension is permanent while revocation is temporary
B) Revocation can only be initiated by the startup itself
C) Suspension can be lifted after rectifying deficiencies; revocation is a final termination
D) Both suspension and revocation have identical legal consequences
አማ  ጥ55  ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ታኃዲሶ እና ስርጭትን ትክክለኛ የሚለይ ሐሳብ የቱ ነው?
ሀ) ታኃዲሶ ቋሚ ሲሆን ስርጭት ጊዜያዊ ነው
ለ) ስርጭት ስታርት አፑ ብቻ ሊጀምረው ይችላል
ሐ) ታኃዲሶ ጉድለቱ ቢስተካከል ሊነሳ ሲችል ስርጭት የቋሚ ስርዘት ነው
መ) ሁለቱም ተመሳሳይ ሕጋዊ ውጤት ያለው
EN  Answer: C) Suspension is reversible upon rectification; revocation is a final termination of the designation (Articles 14 and 15)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ታኃዲሶ ሊስተካከል ሲችል ስርጭት የቋሚ ቅጣት ነው (አዋጆች 14 እና 15)

Q56  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q56  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: A designated startup's business license is suspended by the relevant government body. What happens to its startup designation?
A) Nothing — the business license and startup designation are legally independent
B) The startup designation is automatically suspended without any precondition
C) The startup must notify the Ministry within 10 working days
D) The Ministry reviews the situation before deciding
አማ  ጥ56  ሁኔታ፡ ፈቃዱ በሌላ መንግሥታዊ አካል ታኃዲሶ ሲሆን ምደባው ምን ይሆናል?
ሀ) ምንም — ሙሉ ነፃ ናቸው
ለ) ምደባ ያለ ቅድሚያ ሁኔታ ራሱን ይቋረጣል
ሐ) ስታርት አፑ ከ10 ቀናት ሚኒስቴሩን ያሳውቃል
መ) ሚኒስቴሩ ሁኔታ ካዳኘ ይወስናል
EN  Answer: B) Suspension of a business license by a relevant government organ results in automatic suspension of the designation (Article 14/5)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ፈቃዱ ሲቋረጥ ምደባ ያለ ቅድሚያ ሁኔታ ራሱን ይቋረጣል (አዋጅ 14/5)

Q57  🌐 EN + አማ

B) It is accountable to the Council
C) It has no less than 7 members and is an independent body resolving all designation-related grievances
D) It can revoke startup designations directly without Ministry involvement
አማ  ጥ57  የቅሬታ ኮሚቴ ስለ ትክክለኛ ሁኔታ ምን ሐሳብ ነው?
ሀ) የፋይናንስ ጥያቄዎቹን ብቻ ይዳኛል
ለ) ለምክር ቤቱ ተጠያቂ ነው
ሐ) ቢያንስ 7 አባሎቹ ያሉ ሁሉን ያካተተ ነፃ አካል ሆኖ ምደባ ጥያቄዎቹን ሁሉ ይዳኛል
መ) ምደባ ቀጥታ ሳያሳልፉ ሚኒስቴሩ ሳይኖር ሊሰርዝ ይችላል
EN  Answer: C) The grievance committee has at least 7 members, is inclusive and independent, and resolves grievances including designation, suspension, and cancellation matters (Article 22/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ቅሬታ ኮሚቴ ቢያንስ 7 አባሎቹ ያሉ ሁሉን ያካተተ ነፃ አካል ሆኖ ምደባ፣ ታኃዲሶ እና ስርዘት ቅሬታዎቹን ሁሉ ይዳኛል (አዋጅ 22/1)

Q58  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q58  [Exception Question]  EXCEPTION: Under what circumstance can a LEGALLY REGISTERED organization obtain startup designation even though it has limited business history?
A) It cannot — only unregistered individuals can apply
B) It can apply if it has a valid license and was established for no more than 5 years
C) It can apply only if it has never received government funding
D) It must deregister and reapply as an unregistered entity
አማ  ጥ58  ልዩ ሁኔታ፡ ሕጋዊ ሆኖ ምዝጉ ድርጅት ውስን ሥራ ታሪኩ ቢኖር ምደባ ሊያሰጥ ይችላል?
ሀ) አይችልም — ያልተመዘገቡ ብቻ ሊያመለክቱ ይችላሉ
ለ) ቢያንስ ፈቃድ ያለው ከ5 ዓመት ሳያልፍ ምዝጉ ድርጅት ሊያሰጥ ይችላል
ሐ) ምንም ጊዜ ያለ መንግሥት ድጎማ ካልሆነ
መ) ምዝገባ ሰርዘ ዳቤ ሊያቀርብ ይገባዋል
EN  Answer: B) A registered organization with a valid license established for no more than 5 years can obtain startup designation (Article 7/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቢያንስ ፈቃድ ያለው ከ5 ዓመት ሳያልፍ ምዝጉ ድርጅት ምደባ ሊያሰጥ ይችላል (አዋጅ 7/3)

Q59  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q59  [Application Question]  APPLICATION: The Ministry suspended a startup's designation because it failed to achieve its stated growth objectives. Is this legally correct?
A) Yes — failure to achieve growth objectives is an explicit ground for suspension
B) No — striving to achieve growth objectives is an obligation, but it is not listed as a ground for suspension under Article 14/1
C) Yes — any performance failure is automatically grounds for suspension
D) No — only financial failures lead to suspension
አማ  ጥ59  ትጋት፡ ሚኒስቴሩ ያዳ ስታርት አፕ ዕድገት ዓላማዎቹ ላያሳካ ስለ ምደባ ታኃዲሶ አዋጀ — ትክክል?
ሀ) አዎ — ዕድገት ዓላማ ያለ ማሳካቱ ቀጥታ ምደባ ታኃዲሶ ምክንያት ነው
ለ) አይደለም — ዕድገት ዓላማ ለማሳካቱ ጥረቱ ግዴታ ሆኖ (አዋጅ 12/1/ለ) ነገር ግን ይህ በ14/1 ዝርዝር ታኃዲሶ ምክንያቶቹ ሥር አይካተትም
ሐ) አዎ — ማናቸውም አፈጻጸም ምደባ ታኃዲሶ ይሆናል
መ) አይደለም — ፋይናንሳዊ ጉዳይ ብቻ ናቸው
EN  Answer: B) Failure to achieve growth objectives is not among the specific grounds for suspension in Article 14/1
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ዕድገት ዓላማ ለማሳካቱ ጥረቱ ትክክለኛ ቢሆን ግን በ14/1 ዝርዝር ታኃዲሶ ምክንያቶቹ ሥር አይካተትም

Q60  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q60  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT CORRECTLY describes what happens when the Ministry suspends a startup's designation?
A) The business license is automatically suspended too
B) The Ministry's designation suspension also suspends the business license
C) They are legally independent — the Ministry's designation suspension shall not be construed as a business license suspension
D) A business license suspension always automatically follows a designation suspension
አማ  ጥ60  ሚኒስቴሩ ምደባ ሲያቋርጠው ፈቃዱ ምን ይሆናል?
ሀ) ፈቃዱ ተመሳሳይ ውጤት አለው
ለ) ምደባ ታኃዲሶ ፈቃዱን ይቋርጣል
ሐ) ሙሉ ነፃ ናቸው — ምደባ ታኃዲሶ ፈቃዱን እንደ ቋረጠ አይቆጠርም
መ) ፈቃዱ ቁርጥ ሁሌ ምደባ ታኃዲሶ ቀጥሎ ይቋረጣል
EN  Answer: C) A suspension of startup designation by the Ministry shall not be construed as a suspension of the business license (Article 14/6)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ምደባ ታኃዲሶ ፈቃዱን እንደ ቋረጠ አይቆጠርም (አዋጅ 14/6)


Level 13  —  Hard ⭐⭐⭐
Hard ⭐⭐⭐  |  ከባድ

Q61  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q61  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: An applicant's startup designation application is rejected by the committee. Within how many working days must the Ministry notify the applicant of the rejection and its reason?
A) 15 working days
B) 20 working days
C) 30 working days
D) 45 working days
አማ  ጥ61  ሁኔታ፡ ኮሚቴ ማመልከቻ ውድቅ ቢያደርግ ሚኒስቴሩ ካሳወቀ ምን ያህል ቀናት አሉ?
ሀ) 15 ቀናት
ለ) 20 ቀናት
ሐ) 30 ቀናት
መ) 45 ቀናት
EN  Answer: C) 30 working days (Article 9/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) 30 የሥራ ቀናት (አዋጅ 9/2)

Q62  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q62  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT about the Council's powers regarding the startup grant?
A) The Council directly appoints all committee members without Ministry input
B) The Council endorses the startup grant directive developed by the Ministry of Finance
C) The Council manages the day-to-day operations of the grant program
D) The Council can unilaterally revoke startup designations
አማ  ጥ62  ምክር ቤቱ ስለ ስታርት አፕ ድጎማ ምን ሥልጣን አለው?
ሀ) ሁሉም ኮሚቴ አባሎቹ ሳያሳልፉ ሚኒስቴሩ ሳይኖር ይሾማቸዋል
ለ) ምክር ቤቱ የፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ያዘጋጀ ድጎማ መመሪያ ሃሳቡ ሲሰጥ ትግበራውን ያፈቅዳል
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ዕለታዊ ድጎማ ፕሮግራምን ያስተዳድራል
መ) ምደባ ቀጥታ ሊሰርዝ ይችላል
EN  Answer: B) The Council endorses the startup grant directive developed by the Ministry of Finance and ensures its implementation (Article 5/1/e)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ምክር ቤቱ የፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ያዘጋጀ ድጎማ መመሪያ ሃሳቡ ሰጥቶ ትግበራውን ይፈቅዳል (አዋጅ 5/1/ሠ)

Q63  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q63  [Exception Question]  EXCEPTION: Which sectors are excluded from the 4-year competency certificate grace period for designated startups?
A) Financial services and fintech
B) Technology and software
C) Public health and national security
D) Agricultural and manufacturing
አማ  ጥ63  ልዩ ሁኔታ፡ ለ4 ዓመት ብቃት ምስክር ወረቀት ቅነሳ ሕዝባዊ ጤናና ደህንነት ዘርፎች ለምን ተቀሩ?
ሀ) ዘርፉ ሙሉ ለሙሉ ከአዋጁ ሳይካተት
ለ) ዘርፉ ሕዝብ ሊጎዳ ስለሚችል ብቃት ምስክር ወረቀት ከጅምር ያስፈልጋቸዋል
ሐ) ሕዝብ ጤና ውስጥ ውጭ ኩባንያዎቹ ብዙ ናቸው
መ) ዘርፎቹ ምንም ብቃት ምስክር ወረቀት አያስፈልጋቸውም
EN  Answer: C) Public health and national security sectors are excluded from the grace period (Article 38/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሕዝብን ሊጎዱ ስለሚችሉ ዘርፎቹ ብቃት ምስክር ወረቀቱ ሙሉ ሆኖ ሊኖር ይገባዋል — አዋጅ 38/3 ቀጥታ ከዝርዝሩ ሳይካተቱ

Q64  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q64  [Application Question]  APPLICATION: A startup has been designated for 7 years and now applies for renewal. For how long can the designation remain valid after this renewal?
A) Another full 2-year period — reaching 9 years total
B) Only until the 8-year maximum is reached — so a maximum of 1 more year
C) Another 5 years with Council approval
D) The designation cannot be renewed after 6 years
አማ  ጥ64  ትጋት፡ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ 7 ዓመቱን ሞልቶ ደጅ ሲያቀርብ ምን ያህል ጊዜ ሊሠራ ይችላል?
ሀ) ሌላ 2 ዓመት ሙሉ
ለ) 8 ዓመቱ ጣሪያ ድረስ ብቻ — አንድ ዓመት
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅድ 5 ዓመት
መ) ምደባ ከ6 ዓመቱ ሊታደስ አይችልም
EN  Answer: B) A startup designation cannot stay valid for more than 8 years in total — renewal at year 7 can only extend to the 8-year ceiling (Article 13/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ምደባ ጠቅላላ ከ8 ዓመት ሊበልጥ አይችልም — 7ኛ ዓመቱ ደጅ ሲቀርብ እስከ 8 ዓመቱ ብቻ ሊሠራ ይችላል (አዋጅ 13/4)

ሐ) የንግድ ምዝገባ ስርዘት ምደባ ስርዘት ምክንያት ነው

Q66  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q66  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: A startup is found to have used its designation for unauthorized purposes. What procedure must the Ministry follow BEFORE revoking the designation?
A) The Ministry revokes immediately without prior notice
B) The Ministry must notify the startup in writing to submit its objection
C) The Ministry suspends for 60 days first, then revokes
D) The startup must appear before the grievance committee
አማ  ጥ66  ሁኔታ፡ ምደባ ላልሆነ ዓላማ ሲጠቀሙ ሚኒስቴሩ ሊሰርዝ ቀደም ምን ሂደት አለ?
ሀ) ቀጥታ ሰርዞ ይቀጥላሉ
ለ) ሚኒስቴሩ ስታርት አፑን ምልሃቱ እንዲቀርብ ይነግሩታል
ሐ) መጀመሪያ 60 ቀን ታኃዲሶ ቀጥሎ ስርዘት
መ) ቅሬታ ኮሚቴ ፊት ሊቀርቡ አለባቸው
EN  Answer: B) Before revoking for unauthorized use, the Ministry must notify the startup in writing to submit its objection (Article 15/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሳይሰርዙ ሚኒስቴሩ ስታርት አፑን ምልሃቱ ለሚቀርብ ጊዜ ይነግሩታል (አዋጅ 15/2)

C) They are automatically designated without applying

አማ  ጥ69  ትጋት፡ ይህ አዋጅ ከሚሆን ቀደም ኢንኩቤተር ሲሠሩ ለነበሩ ድርጅቶች ምን ልዩ ሂደት አለ?
ሀ) ደረጃው ሂደቱ ብቻ — ልዩ አይደለም
ለ) ሚኒስቴሩ ቀጣሽ ሂደቱን ያመቻቻቸዋል

B) The Ministry issues a second warning automatically
C) The builder must respond within 30 working days of receipt; non-response exposes the builder to further enforcement including suspension or revocation

አማ  ጥ70  ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ሚኒስቴሩ 30 ቀን አሳወቀ — ምላሽ ካለ ምን ቀጠሎ አለ?
ሀ) ቀጥታ ምደባ ስርዘት
ለ) ሌላ ቁርጥ ቀን ሳይጨምር ሁለተኛ ማስጠንቀቂያ
ሐ) ገንቢው ከ30 ቀን ሳይዘጋ ምላሽ ሊሰጥ ይገባዋል — ካልሰጠ ታኃዲሶ ወይም ስርዘት
መ) ሁሉም ግዴታዎቹ ቁርጥ ካለፈ ይወድቃሉ
EN  Answer: C) The builder must respond within 30 working days (Article 18/3); failure is the basis for further enforcement under Articles 20 or 21
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ገንቢው ከ30 ቀን ሳይዘጋ ምላሽ ሊሰጥ ይገባዋል (አዋጅ 18/3); ካልሰጠ ታኃዲሶ ወይም ስርዘት ሊቀጥሉ ይችላሉ

Q71  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q71  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: A startup co-founder holds 30% of Startup A, which received a grant 6 months ago. This co-founder also co-founded Startup B and wants to apply for a grant through it. Can the application proceed?
A) Yes — because the application is through a different startup entity
B) No — co-founders with 25%+ ownership in a startup that received a grant cannot apply through any startup for 1 year
C) Yes — as long as Startup B is independently designated
D) No — co-founders can never apply for grants through more than one startup at any time
አማ  ጥ71  ሁኔታ፡ የስታርት አፕ ሀ 30% ባለቤት ስለሆነ ሌላ ስታርት አፕ ሊያቀርብ ፈልጓል ጊዜ ዓ 25% ካሎ — ሊቀርብ ይችላል?
ሀ) አዎ — ሌላ ኩባንያ ነው
ለ) አይደለም — ከ25% ሲይዝ ከ1 ዓመቱ ሳይዘጋ ድጎማ ሊያቀርብ አይችልም
ሐ) አዎ — ሁለቱ ሙሉ ነፃ ናቸው
መ) አይደለም — ባለቤቶቹ ሁሌ አይቀርቡም
EN  Answer: B) Co-founders with 25%+ ownership in a startup that received a grant are barred from applying through any startup for 1 year (Article 25/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) 25% ካሎ ስታርት አፕ ሀ ድጎማ ካዳ ባለቤቱ 1 ዓመቱ ሳይሞላ ሌላ ስታርት አፕ ቢሆን ሊያቀርብ አይችልም (አዋጅ 25/4)

Q72  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q72  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT about what a designation certificate for a startup ecosystem builder must include?
A) Name of the startup, founder's name, growth stage, and sector
B) Name of the ecosystem builder, name of the owner(s), classification as ecosystem builder, and sector engaged in
C) Name of the ecosystem builder, annual revenue, and industry classification

አማ  ጥ72  ለኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ምደባ ምስክር ወረቀቱ ምን ያካተተ ሊሆን ይገባዋል?
ሀ) ስታርት አፑ ስሙ፣ ባለቤቱ ስሙ፣ ደረጃ እና ዘርፍ
ለ) ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ስሙ፣ ባለቤቱ/ቷ ስሙ፣ ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ መሆኑ እና ዘርፉ
ሐ) ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ስሙ፣ ዓመታዊ ገቢ እና ዘርፍ
መ) ከስታርት አፕ ምስክር ወረቀቱ ጋር ተመሳሳይ
EN  Answer: B) The ecosystem builder certificate must include: name, owner's name, classification as ecosystem builder, and sector (Article 8/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ስሙ፣ ባለቤቱ ስሙ፣ ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ምደባ እና ዘርፉ (አዋጅ 8/2)

Q73  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q73  [Exception Question]  EXCEPTION: What must the Ministry do BEFORE revoking a designation for unauthorized use?

ሀ) አዎ — ቀጥታ ሂደት
ለ) አይደለም — 15/1/ለ፣ ሐ እና መ ምክንያቶቹ ቅድሚያ ጽሑፋዊ ማሳወቂያ ያስፈልጋሉ
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅድ ብቻ
መ) 60 ቀን ታኃዲሶ ቀደም
EN  Answer: B) For revocations under Article 15/1/b, c, and d, the Ministry must first notify in writing to allow objection (Article 15/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ለ15/1/ለ፣ ሐ እና መ ምክንያቶቹ ሚኒስቴሩ ቅድሚያ ምልሃቱ እንዲቀርብ ጽሑፍ ሊያሳውቅ ይገባዋል (አዋጅ 15/2)

EN  Q75  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT: If a company makes a SIGNIFICANT IMPROVEMENT to an existing designated startup's product, can they obtain a separate designation for that improvement?
A) No — any use of another startup's product is prohibited under Article 41
B) Yes — if the improvement constitutes a significant change that satisfies the definitional requirements of a startup
C) Only with the original startup's written consent
D) Only if the original startup's designation has already expired
አማ  ጥ75  ትክክለኛ ሐሳብ ምንድን ነው — ሌላ ኩባንያ ተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ ምርቱ ላይ ከፍ ያለ ማሻሻያ ቢያደርግ?

ሐ) ቀጣዩ ስታርት አፕ ጽሑፍ ፈቃድ ቢሰጥ ብቻ

Q76  🌐 EN + አማ

C) Foreign employees are never exempt from income tax in Ethiopia

ሀ) ምደባ ቢያልቅ ቢቀር ቅነሳ ቀጥሏ

ሀ) ሁሉም ምደባዎቹ ቀጥታ ሊሰረዙ ዳቤ ሊቀርቡ ይገባቸዋል

C) By April 1, 2026
D) By May 1, 2026
አማ  ጥ83  ጊዜ-መስመር፡ ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ መጋቢት 1 ቀን 2026 ማስጠንቀቂያ ቀቀ — ምላሽ ቀነ-ሰጠ?
ሀ) ሚያዝያ 1 ቀን 2026
ለ) ከ30 ቀናት ሳይዘጋ — ቅዳሜ-እሁድ ሳይቆጠሩ ሚያዝያ አጋሱ
ሐ) ሚያዝያ 2 ቀን 2026
መ) ግንቦት 1 ቀን 2026
EN  Answer: B) The response must be made within 30 working days — approximately mid-April when weekends are excluded (Article 18/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ምላሽ ከ30 የሥራ ቀናት ሳይዘጋ — ቅዳሜ-እሁድ ሳይቆጠሩ ሚያዝያ አጋሱ ሊሆን ይችላሉ (አዋጅ 18/3)

Q84  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q84  [Comparative]  COMPARATIVE: How does the loss carry-forward treatment differ between a startup and an investor who loses money in that startup?
A) Startup: 3 years; Investor: 2 years
B) Startup: 2 years; Investor: 3 years
C) Both carry losses forward for 3 years
D) Only startups can carry forward losses; investors cannot deduct losses
አማ  ጥ84  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ኪሳራ ወደፊት ሸሚቱ (loss carry-forward) ለስታርት አፕ እና ኢንቨስተሩ ምን ይለያቸዋል?
ሀ) ስታርት አፕ 3 ዓመት; ኢንቨስተሩ 2 ዓመት
ለ) ስታርት አፕ 2 ዓመት; ኢንቨስተሩ 3 ዓመት
ሐ) ሁለቱ 3 ዓመት
መ) ስታርት አፖቹ ብቻ ሊሸምቱ ይችላሉ
EN  Answer: A) Startup loss carry-forward = 3 years (Article 31/3); Investor loss carry-forward = 2 years (Article 32/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ) ስታርት አፕ ኪሳራ ወደፊት = 3 ዓመት (አዋጅ 31/3); ኢንቨስተሩ = 2 ዓመት (አዋጅ 32/2)

Q85  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q85  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP: A startup was designated, received a grant, then was legally liquidated. The same founders immediately form a new startup and apply for designation. Can the new startup apply for a grant?
A) No — must wait 2 years after the previous startup's liquidation
B) Yes — legal liquidation removes the one-year restriction on grant reapplication
C) No — founders are permanently barred after a liquidation
D) Yes — but only for a reduced amount approved by the Ministry
አማ  ጥ85  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ቀቀ፣ ድጎማ ቀቀ፣ ሕጋዊ ፈሳሽ ሁኔታ ቀቀ — ቀጥሎ ዳቤ ስታርት አፕ ሊቀርብ ቅደም ምን ቀቀ?
ሀ) 2 ዓመት ቀደም
ለ) ሕጋዊ ፈሳሽ ሁኔታ ከ25/3 እና 25/4 ልክ ያስወግዳቸዋል — ቀጥሎ ሊቀርቡ ይችላሉ
ሐ) ቋሚ ዕቀባ አለ
መ) 50% ልዩ ድጎማ ብቻ
EN  Answer: B) Legal liquidation removes the restrictions under Article 25/3 and 25/4 (Article 25/5)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሕጋዊ ፈሳሽ ሁኔታ ዕቀባዎቹ ያስወግዳቸዋል — ዳቤ ስታርት አፕ ቀጥሎ ሊቀርብ ይችላሉ (አዋጅ 25/5)

Q86  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q86  [Situational]  SITUATIONAL: A startup has been designated for 6 years and successfully renews. How much longer can the designation remain valid after this renewal?
A) Another full 2-year period — for a total of 8 years maximum
B) Another 5 years
C) Indefinitely, as long as all conditions are met
D) Designation cannot be renewed after 6 years
አማ  ጥ86  ሁኔታ፡ ስታርት አፕ 6 ዓመቱን ሞልቶ ተሳካ ምደባ ታደሰ — ምን ያህል ጊዜ ቀጥሎ ሊሠራ ይችላል?
ሀ) ሌላ 2 ዓመት ሙሉ — 8 ዓመቱ ጣሪያ
ለ) ሌላ 5 ዓመት
ሐ) ሁሉም ሁኔታ ቢሟሉ ቢቆዩ
መ) ምደባ ከ6 ዓመቱ ሊታደስ አይቻለውም
EN  Answer: A) The total designation period cannot exceed 8 years — renewal at year 6 can only extend to year 8 (Article 13/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ) ጠቅላላ 8 ዓመት ካለፈ ሊሠሩ አይቻለውም — 6ኛ ዓመቱ ምደባ ሲታደስ 8 ዓመቱ ጣሪያ (አዋጅ 13/4)

ሀ) ቀጥታ ፍርድ ቤት ቤት
ለ) ሚኒስቴሩ ሪፖርት ያቅርቡ — አዋጅ 41/1 ምደባ ሲሰጥ ሚኒስቴሩ ዋናው ሥልጣን ነው

ሐ) ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ብቻ ኮሚቴ ያስፈልጋቸዋል

Q91  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q91  [Situational]  SITUATIONAL: A startup protected under Article 41 finds that another party replicated its designated product and holds a valid patent for similar technology. What is the legal outcome?
A) Article 41/1 absolutely prohibits all replication regardless of IP rights
B) The valid intellectual property right exception applies — the patent holder is exempt from Article 41/1's prohibition
C) Patents cannot override startup designation protection
D) The Ministry must adjudicate between the patent and the designation protection
አማ  ጥ91  ሁኔታ፡ ስታርት አፕ ምርቱን ሊቀዱ ይፈቅዳሉ — ሐሰተኛ ሰነድ ቀቅዶ ያዘ ስብስቡ ቅድሚያ ካዳ ቀጥሎ ምን ይሆናሉ?
ሀ) ቆሞ ምደባ ሸፍኗቸዋል
ለ) ሐሰተኛ IP ቀቅዷቸዋል — 41/2/ለ ልዩ ሁኔታ ቅድሚያ ቀቅዷቸዋል

መ) ሚኒስቴሩ ሁለቱ ሊፈርዱ
EN  Answer: B) A valid intellectual property right is an explicit exception to Article 41/1's protection (Article 41/2/b)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ትክክለኛ አእምሯዊ ንብረት መብቱ ልዩ ሁኔታ ሆኖ 41/1 ዕቀባ ሳይተፈፀምባቸዋ (አዋጅ 41/2/ለ)

አማ  ጥ93  ጊዜ-መስመር፡ ምደባ ቀን 1 ጀምሮ 8 ዓመቱ ጣሪያ ድረስ ስንት 2-ዓመት ምደባ ታደሰ ይቻላቸዋል?

ለ) ሁለቱ 30 ቀናት
ሐ) ስታርት አፕ 30 ቀናት; ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ 45 ቀናት
መ) ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ጊዜ አዋጁ አልቀመጠም
EN  Answer: B) Both must receive a response within 30 working days — Article 9/4 for startups and Article 17/5 for ecosystem builders
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁለቱ 30 ቀናት — ስታርት አፕ (አዋጅ 9/4) እና ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ (አዋጅ 17/5)

Q95  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q95  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP: A startup wants to import capital goods duty-free. What is the correct step-by-step process?
A) Simply import — duty-free is automatic upon designation
B) Submit a list of the capital goods to the Ministry of Finance and obtain approval, then import duty-free
C) Get Ministry of Innovation and Technology approval first, then Ministry of Finance approval, then import
D) Apply through the Digital Startup Portal to receive an import permit
አማ  ጥ95  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ስታርት አፕ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታል ዕቃ ሊያስገባ ትክክለኛ ሂደቱ?
ሀ) ቀጥታ ያስገቡ — ምደባ ሲሰጥ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ቀጥሏ
ለ) ዝርዝሩን ለፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ሲያቀርቡ ፈቃድ ካዳ ቀጥሏ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ያስገቡ
ሐ) ኢቴሚ ፈቃዱ ካዳ ፋይናንስ ካዳ ቀጥሏ ያስገቡ
መ) ፖርታሉ ሊያቀርቡ ፈቃዱ ያስቡ
EN  Answer: B) The startup must submit the list of capital goods to the Ministry of Finance and obtain approval before importing duty-free (Article 33/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ዝርዝሩን ለፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ሲያቀርቡ ፈቃዱ ካዳ ቀጥሏ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ያስገቡ (አዋጅ 33/2)


Level 20  —  Expert ⭐⭐⭐⭐
Expert ⭐⭐⭐⭐  |  ጠቢብ

Q96  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q96  [Case Study]  CASE STUDY: An Ethiopian permanently residing in Dubai prefers treatment as a foreign investor and establishes a startup in Ethiopia that receives designation. What minimum capital requirement applies?
A) The standard minimum capital under Investment Proclamation No. 1180/2020 applies in full
B) No minimum capital requirement under Investment Proclamation No. 1180/2020 applies to this Foreign Startup
C) A reduced minimum of 50% of the standard requirement applies
D) The requirement is waived only if the startup operates in the technology sector
አማ  ጥ96  ጥናት፡ ዱባይ ቋሚ ነዋሪ ኢትዮጵያዊ ውጭ ኢንቨስተሩ ሆኖ ኢትዮጵያ ስታርት አፕ ሲያቋቁም ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ?
ሀ) ኢንቨስትመንት አዋጅ 1180/2020 ሙሉ ደረጃ
ለ) ኢንቨስትመንት አዋጅ 1180/2020 ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ለዚህ ስታርት አፕ አይሠራም
ሐ) 50% ቅነሳ
መ) ቴክኖሎጂ ዘርፍ ብቻ ቅነሳ
EN  Answer: B) The minimum capital requirement under Investment Proclamation No. 1180/2020 does not apply to Foreign Startups (Article 40/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ኢንቨስትመንት አዋጅ 1180/2020 ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ለዚህ የውጭ ስታርት አፕ አይሠራም (አዋጅ 40/1)

Q97  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q97  [What Happens Next?]  WHAT HAPPENS NEXT: A startup ecosystem builder's designation is revoked under the proclamation. What specific obligation does the ecosystem builder have immediately after revocation?
A) No obligations remain — the relationship ends immediately
B) The ecosystem builder must submit a mandatory exit report
C) The ecosystem builder must return all grants received to the Ministry
D) The ecosystem builder must notify all designated startups it was supporting
አማ  ጥ97  ቀጥሎ ምን ይሆናሉ፡ ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ምደባ ሲሰረዝ ምን ቀዳሚ ግዴታ አለ?
ሀ) ምንም ግዴታ አይቀርም
ለ) ግዴታ ሪፖርቱ ሊያቀርቡ ይገባቸዋሉ
ሐ) ሁሉም ድጎማዎቹ ሊመለሱ ይገባቸዋሉ
መ) ሰዎቻቸዋን ሊያሳውቁ ይገባቸዋሉ
EN  Answer: B) The ecosystem builder is required to submit a mandatory exit report (Article 21/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ሳለ ሪፖርቱ ሊያቀርቡ ይገባቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 21/2)

Q98  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q98  [Timeline & Comparative]  TIMELINE & COMPARATIVE: Compare the response timelines for startup designation applications vs. startup ecosystem builder designation applications. Are they the same?
A) Startup: 20 working days; Ecosystem Builder: 30 working days
B) Both must be responded to within 30 working days
C) Startup: 30 working days; Ecosystem Builder: 45 working days
D) The proclamation specifies no timeline for ecosystem builder applications
አማ  ጥ98  ጊዜ-ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ምላሽ ጊዜ እና ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ምደባ ምላሽ ጊዜ ተመሳሳይ ናቸው?
ሀ) ስታርት አፕ 20 ቀናት; ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ 30 ቀናት
ለ) ሁለቱ 30 ቀናት
ሐ) ስታርት አፕ 30 ቀናት; ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ 45 ቀናት
መ) ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ ጊዜ አዋጁ አልቀመጠም
EN  Answer: B) Both must receive a response within 30 working days — Article 9/4 for startups and Article 17/5 for ecosystem builders
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁለቱ 30 ቀናት — ስታርት አፕ (አዋጅ 9/4) እና ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ (አዋጅ 17/5)

Q99  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q99  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: A startup wants to import capital goods duty-free under Article 33. After receiving the goods, it wants to sell the machines to a competitor. What restrictions apply?
A) The startup can sell freely — the duty-free benefit was already granted
B) The startup must obtain prior Ministry of Finance approval before selling or transferring any duty-free imported capital goods
C) The startup must pay back only the customs duty waived and can then sell freely
D) The startup needs Ministry of Innovation and Technology approval to sell
አማ  ጥ99  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታል ዕቃ ካዘዘ ቀጥሏ ለሌላ ሊሸጥ ፈልጓ — ምን ዕቀባ አለ?
ሀ) ሊሸጡ ይቻሏቸዋል — ቀረጥ-ነፃ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ
ለ) ቅድሚያ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ ሳያሰቡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዕቃዎቹ ሊሸጡ ወይም ሊዛወሩ አይቻሏቸዋም
ሐ) ቀረጡ ተከፍሎ ቀጥሏ ሊሸጡ
መ) ኢቴሚ ፈቃዱ ያስፈልጋቸዋሉ
EN  Answer: B) A startup cannot sell, transfer, or dispose of duty-free imported capital goods without prior Ministry of Finance approval (Article 33/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቅድሚያ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ ሳያሰቡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዕቃዎቹ ሊሸጡ ወይም ሊዛወሩ አይቻሏቸዋም (አዋጅ 33/3)

Q100  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q100  [Case Study]  CASE STUDY: A designated startup has both Ethiopian and foreign national employees. The designation expires in 6 months. What are the correct tax implications for each group?
A) Both groups lose tax exemptions immediately upon receiving expiry notification
B) Foreign nationals: exempt from income tax until designation expires; Ethiopian employees: follow standard Ethiopian tax rules
C) Both groups are exempt from income tax for 1 year after designation expires
D) Only Ethiopian employees receive tax benefits under this proclamation
አማ  ጥ100  ጥናት፡ ተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ ኢትዮጵያዊ እና ውጭ ዜጋ ሠራተኞቹ አሉ። ምደባ ከ6 ወር ቀደም ሊያልቅ ነው። ለሁለቱ ቡድኖቹ ምን ስለ ግብር ቀጥሏ?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ቡድኖቹ ቀጥሏ ቅነሳቸዋቸዋሉ
ለ) ውጭ ዜጎቹ ምደባ ሳይሞላ ቅነሳቸዋ; ኢትዮጵያዊያኑ ደረጃው ታሪፍ
ሐ) ሁለቱ ቡድኖቹ 1 ዓመቱ ቀጥሏ ቅነሳቸዋሉ
መ) ኢትዮጵያዊያኑ ብቻ ቅነሳ ያሉ
EN  Answer: B) Article 30/4 specifically exempts income earned by foreign nationals before designation expiry; Ethiopian employees follow standard tax rules (Article 30/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) አዋጅ 30/4 ምደባ ሳይሞላ ውጭ ዜጎቹ ነፃ ሲሆኑ ኢትዮጵያዊያኑ ደረጃው ታሪፍ ይፈፅሙ

Q101  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q101  [Multiple Choice]  What is the purpose of the Startup Grant Program?
A) To provide loans to startups at subsidized interest rates
B) To provide financial support to startups and startup ecosystem builders for their growth and development
C) To fund government technology projects
D) To attract foreign investment into Ethiopia
አማ  ጥ101  የስታርት አፕ ድጎማ ፕሮግራም ዓላማ ምንድን ነው?
ሀ) ለስታርት አፖች በድጎማ ወለድ ብድር ለማቅረብ
ለ) ለስታርት አፖች እና ስታርት አፕ ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢዎች ዕድገታቸውን ለማሳካት ፋይናንሳዊ ድጋፍ ለማቅረብ
ሐ) የመንግሥት ቴክኖሎጂ ፕሮጀክቶችን ለመደገፍ
መ) የውጭ ኢንቨስትመንት ወደ ኢትዮጵያ ለመሳብ
EN  Answer: B) To provide financial support to startups and startup ecosystem builders for their growth and development (Article 23/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ፕሮግራሙ ለስታርት አፖች እና ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢዎች ዕድገታቸውን ለሚያስፈልጉ ፋይናንሳዊ ድጋፍ ያቀርባል (አዋጅ 23/1)

Q102  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q102  [True / False]  True or False: A grant received under this proclamation must be repaid after the startup becomes profitable.
አማ  ጥ102  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ በዚህ አዋጅ መሠረት የሚቀበሉት ድጎማ ስታርት አፑ ትርፋማ ከሆነ በኋላ ተመላሽ መደረግ አለበት።
EN  Answer: False — a 'Grant' is a non-refundable transfer of money provided to a startup or ecosystem builder (Article 2/12).
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — 'ድጎማ' ማለት ተመላሽ-ሰጪ ያልሆነ ገንዘብ ልውውጥ ሲሆን ለስታርት አፕ ወይም ኢኮሲስተም ገንቢ የሚሰጥ ነው (አዋጅ 2/12)።

Q103  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q103  [Multiple Choice]  The Startup Grant Program operates under the oversight of the Council. Which institution is responsible for its day-to-day management?
A) The Ministry of Finance
B) The Ministry of Innovation and Technology
C) The National Startup Designation Committee
D) The National Bank of Ethiopia
አማ  ጥ103  የስታርት አፕ ድጎማ ፕሮግራም በምክር ቤቱ ቁጥጥር ሥር ማን ነው የሚያስተዳድረው?
ሀ) የፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴር
ለ) የኢኖቬሽን እና ቴክኖሎጂ ሚኒስቴር
ሐ) ብሔራዊ የስታርት አፕ ምደባ ኮሚቴ
መ) ብሔራዊ ባንክ ኢትዮጵያ
EN  Answer: B) The Ministry of Innovation and Technology manages the Grant Program under Council oversight (Article 23/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) የኢኖቬሽን እና ቴክኖሎጂ ሚኒስቴር ፕሮግራሙን በምክር ቤቱ ቁጥጥር ሥር ያስተዳድራል (አዋጅ 23/2)

Q104  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q104  [True / False]  True or False: A startup can use a received grant to pay dividends to its shareholders.
አማ  ጥ104  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ አንድ ስታርት አፕ የሚቀበለውን ድጎማ ለባለቤቶቹ ትርፍ ለመክፈል መጠቀም ይችላል።
EN  Answer: False — paying dividends is an explicit prohibited use of grants (Article 24/2/d).
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ትርፍ ክፍፍል ለድጎማ ዓላማ ፈጽሞ የተከለከለ ነው (አዋጅ 24/2/ዲ)።

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) የፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴር (አዋጅ 23/4)

Q106  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q106  [Multiple Choice]  Which of the following is a PERMITTED use of a startup grant?
A) Repaying personal debts of the founders
B) Acquiring equipment and technology for the startup's operations
C) Paying dividends to shareholders
D) Making investments in other companies
አማ  ጥ106  ለድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ ምን ሥርዓት ተፈቅዷቸዋሉ?
ሀ) ባለቤቶቹ ዕዳ መክፈያ
ለ) ቴክኖሎጂ እና መሳሪያ ቅጥር
ሐ) ትርፍ ማዛወሪያ ለባለቤቶቹ
መ) ሌሎች ኩባንያዎቹ ኢንቨስትመንት
EN  Answer: B) Acquiring equipment and technology (Article 24/1/b)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቴክኖሎጂ እና መሳሪያ ቅጥር ፈቅዷቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 24/1/ለ)

Q107  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q107  [True / False]  True or False: A startup that received a grant can apply for another grant after 6 months.
አማ  ጥ107  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ ድጎማ ያዘ ስታርት አፕ ከ6 ወር ቀጥሎ ዳቤ ሊያቀርቡ ይቻሏቸዋሉ።
EN  Answer: False — a startup that received a grant cannot apply for another grant for at least 1 year (Article 25/3).
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ድጎማ ያዘ ስታርት አፕ ዳቤ ሳያቀርቡ 1 ዓመቱ ሊሞላ ይገባዋቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 25/3)።

ሐ) 300,000 ብር (30%)

ለ) አይደለም — ሙሉ-ጊዜ ሠራተኞቹ ብቻ ሽፈኛ
ሐ) አዎ — ኢንተርን ጨምሮ ሠራተኞቹ ቅጥር ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
መ) ኢንተርን ቀጥሎ ሙሉ ሠራተኛ ካዳ ብቻ
EN  Answer: C) Hiring and training of employees, including internship expenses, are explicitly listed as a permitted use of grant funds (Article 24/1/e)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ሠራተኞቹ ቅጥር እና ሥልጠና ፍጽሞ ኢንተርን ዋጋ ጨምሮ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 24/1/ሠ)

Q124  🌐 EN + አማ

EN  Answer: True (Article 32/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ እውነት (አዋጅ 32/1)

(4) Fulfil requirements set in the startup grant directive
አማ  ጥ127  ስታርት አፕ ድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ ብቁ ለማዕቀፍ ቅደም ተከተሉ:

Q133  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q133  [Match]  Match each restriction to the correct duration or scope:
• Post-grant waiting period before reapplying: _____
• Loss carry-forward period (startup): _____
• Loss carry-forward period (investor): _____
አማ  ጥ133  እያንዳንዱ ዕቀባ ጊዜ ምን ናቸዋሉ?

• ስታርት አፕ ኪሳራ ወደፊት: _____
• ኢንቨስተሩ ኪሳራ ወደፊት: _____
EN  Answer: Post-grant waiting period = 1 year (Art. 25/3); Startup loss carry-forward = 3 years (Art. 31/3); Investor loss carry-forward = 2 years (Art. 32/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ዳቤ ቀደም ጊዜ = 1 ዓመቱ (አዋጅ 25/3); ስታርት አፕ ኪሳራ = 3 ዓመቱ (አዋጅ 31/3); ኢንቨስተሩ ኪሳራ = 2 ዓመቱ (አዋጅ 32/2)

Q138  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q138  [Match]  Match each incentive to its scope:
• Income tax exemption for foreign national employees: _____
• Income tax exemption for startup income: _____
• Capital gains tax exemption for startup investors: _____
አማ  ጥ138  እያንዳንዱ ቅነሳ ምን ሽፈኛ?
• ውጭ ዜጋ ሠራተኛ ቀረጥ: _____
• ስታርት አፕ ትርፍ ቀረጥ: _____
• ካፒታሉ ዕድሉ ዋጋ ቀረጥ: _____
EN  Answer: Foreign national employees = during designation period (Art. 30/4); Startup income = during designation period (Art. 30/2); Capital gains = for investors in startups (Art. 30/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ውጭ ዜጋ ሠራተኛ = ምደባ ጊዜ (አዋጅ 30/4); ስታርት አፕ ትርፍ = ምደባ ጊዜ (አዋጅ 30/2); ካፒታሉ ዋጋ = ስታርት አፕ ኢንቨስቶቹ (አዋጅ 30/3)

Q139  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q139  [Multiple Answers]  Which of the following are co-founders' obligations that affect grant eligibility? (Select ALL that apply)

D) Maintain a minimum 10% ownership stake in the applying startup

ሐ) ትዳር ወይም ቤተሰቦቹ ባለቤቶቹ ያሳውቁ
መ) 10% ወሰን ያዘ
EN  Answer: A, B, and C — there is no 10% minimum ownership disclosure requirement for co-founders (Article 25/7)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ፣ ለ እና ሐ — 10% ወሰን ያዘ አልተዘርዘረም (አዋጅ 25/7)

Q144  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q144  [Multiple Answers]  A startup co-founder holds 26% in Startup A that just received a grant, and 24% in Startup B. Which statement is CORRECT? (Select ALL that apply)
A) The co-founder's 24%-owned Startup B is barred from applying for a grant for 1 year
B) The co-founder's 24%-owned Startup B can apply for a grant
C) The 25% threshold is the trigger; the co-founder's 26% ownership in Startup A qualifies them as a founder for this restriction
D) The restriction applies only to the specific startup that received the grant
አማ  ጥ144  ስታርት አፕ ሀ ባለቤቶቹ 26% ያዙ ቀጥሏ 6 ወር ቀደም ድጎማ ያዙ፤ ስታርት አፕ ለ ባለቤቶቹ 24% ያዙ — ስታርት አፕ ለ ሊቀርቡ ይቻሏቸዋሉ?
ሀ) አይደለም — ስታርት አፕ ሀ 25%+ ዕቀባ ሸፍኖ ለስታርት አፕ ለ ሁሉ
ለ) አዎ — ስታርት አፕ ለ 24% ወሰን አልፈም
ሐ) ስታርት አፕ ሀ ዕቀባ ወሰን 25%; 26% ፈቀደ
መ) ዕቀባ ስታርት አፕ ሀ ብቻ
EN  Answer: B and C — the 25%+ trigger applies to the co-founder in the grant-receiving startup. The 24% stake in Startup B is below the threshold, so Startup B may apply
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ እና ሐ — 25%+ ወሰን ስታርት አፕ ሀ ሲሸፍን ስታርት አፕ ለ 24% ወሰን ሳይዘጋ ሊቀርቡ ይቻሏቸዋሉ

Q151  🌐 EN + አማ

B) The application is rejected — a startup cannot apply for another grant until 1 full year has passed
C) The application is accepted with a reduced grant amount
D) The application is reviewed by the Council for exceptional approval
አማ  ጥ151  ሁኔታ፡ ስታርት አፕ 11 ወር ቀደም ዳቤ ሊያቀርቡ ፈልጉ — ምን ውጤቱ?

ለ) ሊቀርቡ አይቻሏቸዋም — 1 ዓመቱ ሳይሞላ
ሐ) ዝቅ ድጎማ ቢቀርቡ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ

Q152  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q152  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT about the treatment of a startup's operating losses after designation expires?

B) Losses incurred during the designation period can be carried forward for 3 years after the designation expires
C) Losses can be carried forward indefinitely after designation expires
D) Only losses from the last year of designation can be carried forward
አማ  ጥ152  ምደባ ካለቀ ቀጥሏ ስታርት አፕ ኪሳራ ወደፊት ምን?
ሀ) ሁሉ ኪሳራ ምደባ ካለቀ
ለ) ምደባ ጊዜ ኪሳራ 3 ዓመቱ ምደባ ካለቀ ቀጥሏ ወደፊት

መ) ምደባ ካለቀ ኪሳራ ቀጥሏ ሊሸሙ

Q153  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q153  [Exception Question]  EXCEPTION: Under what specific circumstance does the 1-year grant reapplication restriction NOT apply?

D) If the startup received only 50% of the original grant amount
አማ  ጥ153  ልዩ ሁኔታ፡ ሕጋዊ ፈሳሽ ሁኔታ 1 ዓመቱ ዕቀባ ሳያስወግዱ ምን?

D) The application is reviewed by the Council on a case-by-case basis
አማ  ጥ154  ትጋት፡ ውጭ ስታርት አፕ ድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ ሊቀርቡ ፈልጉ — ምን ውጤቱ?
ሀ) ምደባ ያዙ ስለሆነ ቀጥሏ
ለ) ዝቅ ድጎማ ቢቀርቡ
ሐ) ሊቀርቡ አይቻሏቸዋም — ውጭ ስታርት አፖቹ ድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ ሳይካተቱ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: C) Foreign startups are explicitly excluded from the Startup Grant Program (Article 40/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ውጭ ስታርት አፖቹ ድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ ሳይካተቱ (አዋጅ 40/3)

EN  Q159  [Application Question]  APPLICATION: A startup incurs a 500,000 ETB loss during its final year of designation. It earns 150,000 ETB profit in year 1 after expiry. How much loss remains to carry forward?

B) 350,000 ETB
C) 0 ETB
D) Carry-forward is unlimited
አማ  ጥ159  ትጋት፡ ስታርት አፕ 500,000 ብር ኪሳራ ካዱ 1 ዓመቱ ቀጥሏ 150,000 ብር ትርፍ ካዱ ቀሪ?

ሐ) 0 ብር
መ) ያለ ገደቡ
EN  Answer: B) 500,000 − 150,000 = 350,000 ETB remaining, to be carried into year 2 (Article 31/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) 500,000 − 150,000 = 350,000 ብር ቀሪ (አዋጅ 31/3)

EN  Q166  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: A co-founder with 30% in Startup A applies for a grant through Startup B where she holds 20%. Startup A received a grant 6 months ago. Can Startup B apply?

B) Yes — Startup B is an independent legal entity
C) Yes — the 20% in Startup B is below the 25% threshold
D) Yes — only Startup A is restricted
አማ  ጥ166  ሁኔታ፡ ባለቤቶቹ 30% ስታርት አፕ ሀ ያዙ — ቀጥሏ 6 ወር — ስታርት አፕ ለ 20% ያዙ — ስታርት አፕ ለ ሊቀርቡ?
ሀ) አይደለም — ስታርት አፕ ሀ 25%+ ዕቀባ ለሁሉ ስታርት አፖቹ
ለ) አዎ — ሌላ ኩባንያ
ሐ) አዎ — ስታርት አፕ ለ 20% ወሰን ሳይዘጋ
መ) አዎ — ስታርት አፕ ሀ ብቻ ዕቀባ
EN  Answer: A) A co-founder holding 25%+ in a startup that received a grant cannot apply through any startup for 1 year (Article 25/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ) ስታርት አፕ ሀ 25%+ ያዙ ቀጥሏ 1 ዓመቱ ሳይሞላ ሁሉ ስታርት አፖቹ ቀጥሏ ዳቤ ሊቀርቡ አይቻሏቸዋም (አዋጅ 25/4)

C) Yes — but only if the intern is a foreign national
D) Only with prior Ministry of Labor approval
አማ  ጥ168  ልዩ ሁኔታ፡ ስታርት አፕ ድጎማ ሥሩ ኢንተርን ሠሩ — ሕጋዊ?
ሀ) አይቻሏቸዋም
ለ) አዎ — ኢንተርን ዋጋ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
ሐ) ውጭ ዜጋ ኢንተርን ብቻ
መ) ሥራ ሚኒስቴሩ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: B) Internship expenses are explicitly permitted under Article 24/1/e
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ኢንተርን ዋጋ ጨምሮ ቅጥር ሥልጠና ፈቅዷቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 24/1/ሠ)

B) 1,500,000 ETB
C) 0 ETB
D) 2,000,000 ETB

ሐ) 0 ብር
መ) 2,000,000 ብር
EN  Answer: A) 2,000,000 − 500,000 (Y1) = 1,500,000; 1,500,000 − 800,000 (Y2) = 700,000 ETB remaining (Articles 32/1 and 32/2)

EN  Q183  [Timeline]  TIMELINE: A startup's designation expires December 31, 2027. It has accumulated losses of 1,200,000 ETB. In what year does the 3-year carry-forward expire?
A) December 31, 2028
B) December 31, 2029
C) December 31, 2030
D) December 31, 2031
አማ  ጥ183  ጊዜ-መስመር፡ ምደባ ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2027 ካለቀ 1,200,000 ብር ኪሳራ ቀሪ — ወደፊት ቀን?
ሀ) ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2028
ለ) ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2029
ሐ) ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2030
መ) ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2031
EN  Answer: C) 3 years from December 31, 2027 = December 31, 2030 (Article 31/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) 3 ዓመቱ ከታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2027 = ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2030 (አዋጅ 31/3)

Q184  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q184  [Comparative]  COMPARATIVE: How does the income tax treatment of a designated startup's income differ from an Ethiopian employee working in that startup?
A) Both the startup's income and Ethiopian employees' income are exempt
B) The startup's income is exempt from income tax; Ethiopian employees pay standard income tax
C) Both are subject to income tax at a reduced rate
D) Only the startup's income is taxed; employees are exempt
አማ  ጥ184  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ ትርፍ ቀረጥ እና ኢትዮጵያዊ ሠራተኛ ቀረጥ ምን?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ቀረጥ ነፃ
ለ) ስታርት አፕ ትርፍ ቀረጥ ነፃ; ኢትዮጵያዊ ሠራተኛ ደረጃው ቀረጥ
ሐ) ሁለቱ ዝቅ ቀረጥ
መ) ኢትዮጵያዊ ሠራተኛ ብቻ ቀረጥ ነፃ
EN  Answer: B) Article 30/2 exempts the startup's income; Article 30/4 exempts only foreign national employees — Ethiopian employees follow standard tax (Article 30)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) አዋጅ 30/2 ስታርት አፕ ትርፍ ምደባ ጊዜ ነፃ; ኢትዮጵያዊ ሠራተኛ ደረጃው ቀረጥ — አዋጅ 30/4 ውጭ ዜጋ ሠራተኛ ብቻ ነፃ

Q185  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q185  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: Two startups are co-owned by three founders each holding exactly 33.33% in both. Startup A received a grant 4 months ago. Can Startup B apply for a grant now?
A) Yes — they are independent legal entities
B) No — each co-founder holds more than 25% in Startup A; all are barred from applying through any startup for 1 year
C) Yes — only founders holding over 50% are restricted
D) Only with Ministry exception approval
አማ  ጥ185  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ሁለቱ ስታርት አፖቹ ሦስቱ ባለቤቶቹ 33.33% ሁለቱ ያዙ — ስታርት አፕ ሀ 4 ወር ቀደም ድጎማ ያዘ — ስታርት አፕ ለ ሊቀርቡ?
ሀ) አዎ — ሌላ ኩባንያ
ለ) አይቻሏቸዋም — ሁሉ ባለቤቶቹ 25%+ ስታርት አፕ ሀ ያዙ — 1 ዓመቱ ሳይሞላ
ሐ) አዎ — 50%+ ብቻ ዕቀባ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: B) All three co-founders hold more than 25% in Startup A; the restriction bars any startup in which they co-found from applying for 1 year (Article 25/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ ሦስቱ ባለቤቶቹ 25%+ ስታርት አፕ ሀ ያዙ — 1 ዓመቱ ሳይሞላ ሁሉ ስታርት አፖቻቸዋቸዋ ዳቤ ሊቀርቡ አይቻሏቸዋም (አዋጅ 25/4)

Q186  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q186  [Situational]  SITUATIONAL: A startup uses 25% on salaries, 15% on workspace, 20% on research, and transfers 40% to a related startup owned by a sibling of the founders. Is this lawful?
A) Yes — all within permitted caps
B) No — transferring grant funds to another company is prohibited under Article 24/2/b
C) Yes — related party transfers are permitted with disclosure
D) Only if the sibling's startup is also designated
አማ  ጥ186  ሁኔታ፡ 14 ወር ቀደም ድጎማ ያዘ — አዲስ ሃሳብ ቀጥሏ ዳቤ ሊቀርቡ?
ሀ) አይቻሏቸዋም — አንድ ጊዜ ብቻ ሕይወቱ
ለ) አዎ — 14 ወር 1 ዓመቱ ሞልቶ አዲስ ሃሳቡ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
ሐ) አዎ — ስኬለኛ ስለሆኑ ብቁ አይደሉም
መ) 50% ዝቅ ብቻ
EN  Answer: B) Transferring grants to another company (even a related one) constitutes investing in another company — explicitly prohibited (Article 24/2/b)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) 14 ወር 1 ዓመቱ ሞልቶ አዲስ ሃሳቡ ዳቤ ሊቀርቡ ይቻሏቸዋሉ (አዋጆች 25/3 እና 25/6)

B) Guarantee Fund reduces loan risk by guaranteeing loans; Fund of Funds invests in VC/PE funds that then invest in startups
C) Guarantee Fund invests equity; Fund of Funds provides loan guarantees

አማ  ጥ189  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ዋስትና ፈንዱ እና ፈንዱ-ኦፍ-ፈንዱ ምን?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ቀጥታ ድጎማ ሰጡ
ለ) ዋስትና ፈንዱ ብድር ዋስ; ፈንዱ-ኦፍ-ፈንዱ ቬንቸር ካፒታሉ ቀጥሏ ስታርት አፖቹ ኢንቨስቱ
ሐ) ዋስትና ፈንዱ ኢኩዊቲ ኢንቨስቱ; ፈንዱ-ኦፍ-ፈንዱ ዋስ
መ) ሁለቱ ኢቴሚ ያስተዳድሩ

EN  Q190  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP: A startup earns 500,000 ETB profit during designation and a 200,000 ETB capital gain from selling assets. How are these treated for tax purposes?
A) Both are taxable income
B) The 500,000 ETB profit is exempt from income tax during designation; the 200,000 ETB capital gain is subject to standard tax rules unless specifically exempted — Art. 30/3 exempts capital gains for investors, not the startup's own asset sales
C) Both are fully exempt
D) Only the capital gain is taxable
አማ  ጥ190  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ጊዜ 500,000 ብር ትርፍ ያዘ — ካፒታሉ ሸጡ 200,000 ብር ትርፍ ያዘ — ሁለቱ ቀረጥ ምን?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ቀረጥ
ለ) 500,000 ብር ትርፍ ምደባ ጊዜ ቀረጥ ነፃ; 200,000 ብር ካፒታሉ ዋጋ ደረጃው ቀረጥ ሥሩ — አዋጅ 30/3 ኢንቨስቶቹ ብቻ
ሐ) ሁለቱ ሙሉ ቀረጥ ነፃ
መ) ካፒታሉ ዋጋ ብቻ ቀረጥ
EN  Answer: B) Article 30/2 exempts startup income during designation; Article 30/3 provides capital gains exemption for INVESTORS in startups — the startup's own asset-sale capital gain is not covered by Art. 30/3
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) አዋጅ 30/2 ስታርት አፕ ትርፍ ምደባ ጊዜ ቀረጥ ነፃ; አዋጅ 30/3 ካፒታሉ ዋጋ ቅነሳ ኢንቨስቶቹ ብቻ — ስታርት አፑ ካፒታሉ ዕድሉ ዋጋ ሌሎቹ ደረጃው ቀረጥ ሥሩ

Q194  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q194  [Comparative]  COMPARATIVE: How does the tax exemption for a startup's income compare with the exemption for grants received?
A) Both are exempt only during the designation period
B) Startup income is exempt during designation; grants received are exempt from income tax without being tied to the designation period
C) Grants are fully taxable; startup income is exempt
D) Both are permanently exempt
አማ  ጥ194  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ስታርት አፕ ትርፍ ቀረጥ እና ዳቢ ቀረጥ ምን?

ለ) ስታርት አፕ ትርፍ ምደባ ጊዜ ቀረጥ ነፃ; ዳቢ ቀረጥ ምደባ ጊዜ ሳይዘጋ ቀረጥ ነፃ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) አዋጅ 30/2 ስታርት አፕ ትርፍ ምደባ ጊዜ ቀረጥ ነፃ; አዋጅ 30/1 ዳቢ ቀረጥ ምደባ ጊዜ ሳይዘጋ ቀረጥ ነፃ

Q195  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q195  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: A founder holds 26% in Startup A (received grant 3 months ago), 30% in Startup B, and 5% in Startup C. Which startups are barred from applying for a grant?
A) Only Startup A is barred
B) Startup B only
C) Both Startup B and Startup C
D) Startup B (30% > 25%) is barred; Startup C (5% < 25%) is not barred
አማ  ጥ195  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ባለቤቶቹ ሀ 26% ስታርት አፕ ሀ ያዙ (3 ወር ቀደም ድጎማ)፣ 30% ስታርት አፕ ለ፣ 5% ስታርት አፕ ሐ — ምን ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ስታርት አፕ ሀ ብቻ
ለ) ስታርት አፕ ለ ብቻ
ሐ) ሁለቱ ስታርት አፕ ለ እና ሐ
መ) ስታርት አፕ ለ (30% > 25%) ዕቀባ; ስታርት አፕ ሐ (5% < 25%) ዕቀባ ሳይሆን
EN  Answer: D) Startup B: 30% > 25% threshold → barred. Startup C: 5% < 25% → not barred under Article 25/4's co-founder definition (Articles 25/4 and 7/2/b)
አማ  መልስ፡ መ) ስታርት አፕ ለ 30% > 25% → ዕቀባ; ስታርት አፕ ሐ 5% < 25% → ዕቀባ ሳይሆን (አዋጆች 25/4 እና 7/2/ለ)


Level 20  —  Expert ⭐⭐⭐⭐
Expert ⭐⭐⭐⭐  |  ጠቢብ

Q196  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q196  [Case Study]  CASE STUDY: A startup receives 4,000,000 ETB. It allocates: 1,200,000 ETB (30%) to salaries, 800,000 ETB (20%) to workspace, 1,000,000 ETB to research, 300,000 ETB to raw materials, and 700,000 ETB to dividend payments. Identify all violations and consequences.
A) No violations — all uses are justified
B) The 700,000 ETB dividend payment is prohibited; it must be returned per Article 26, and the startup faces legal enforcement
C) The 1,200,000 ETB salary allocation violates the 30% cap
D) The 800,000 ETB workspace allocation violates the 20% cap
አማ  ጥ196  ጥናት፡ ስታርት አፕ 4,000,000 ብር ድጎማ ያዘ — 1,200,000 ደሞዝ (30%)፣ 800,000 ቦታ (20%)፣ 1,000,000 ምርምር፣ 300,000 ጥሬ ዕቃ፣ 700,000 ትርፍ ክፍፍሉ — ጥፋቶቹ ምን?
ሀ) ምንም ጥፋቱ
ለ) 700,000 ትርፍ ክፍፍሉ ፈጽሞ ከልክሏቸዋሉ — አዋጅ 26 ሥሩ ሊመለሱ
ሐ) 1,200,000 ደሞዝ 30% ዕቀባ ጥሷቸዋ
መ) 800,000 ቦታ 20% ዕቀባ ጥሷቸዋ
EN  Answer: B) The 700,000 ETB dividend payment is the only violation (Art. 24/2/d). Salaries (30%) and workspace (20%) are within permitted caps. Research and raw materials are permitted uses.
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) 700,000 ትርፍ ክፍፍሉ ፈጽሞ ከልክሏቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 24/2/ደ). ደሞዝ (30%) እና ቦታ (20%) ወሰን ሳይዘጉ ናቸዋሉ

Q197  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q197  [What Happens Next?]  WHAT HAPPENS NEXT: A startup applies for a grant and lists three other startups where it has ownership. The Ministry discovers a fourth undisclosed startup where the co-founder holds 26%. What is the consequence?
A) The application is accepted
B) Failure to disclose violates Article 25/7 — the Ministry can reject the application and take enforcement action
C) Only a warning is issued for the first offense
D) The grant amount is reduced proportionally
አማ  ጥ197  ቀጥሎ ምን ይሆናሉ፡ ባለቤቶቹ 3 ስታርት አፖቹ ዘርዝሩ 4ኛ ሳያሳውቁ — ምን?
ሀ) ዳቤ ቀጥሏ
ለ) አዋጅ 25/7 ሙሉ ማሳወቂያ — ካልሰጡ ዳቤ ሳቢ እና ሌሎቹ ዕቀባ
ሐ) ማስጠንቀቂያ ብቻ
መ) ያሳወቁ ዳቤ ሲቀጥሉ ሳያሳወቁ ስርዘት
EN  Answer: B) Article 25/7 mandates full disclosure of all startups with ownership or significant management roles — failure to disclose is a material violation
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) አዋጅ 25/7 ሙሉ ማሳወቂያ ሲጠይቅ — ካልሰጡ ጥሰት

Q198  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q198  [Timeline]  TIMELINE: A startup's designation was valid from January 1, 2020 to December 31, 2027 (8 years maximum). It accumulated losses in years 6, 7, and 8. By what date must it fully utilize the loss carry-forward benefit?
A) December 31, 2028
B) December 31, 2029
C) December 31, 2030
D) December 31, 2031
አማ  ጥ198  ጊዜ-መስመር፡ ምደባ ጥር 1 ቀን 2020 – ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2027 (8 ዓመቱ ጣሪያ) — 6፣ 7 እና 8 ዓመቱ ኪሳራ — ወደፊት ቀን?
ሀ) ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2028
ለ) ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2029
ሐ) ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2030
መ) ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2031
EN  Answer: C) 3 years after designation expiry (December 31, 2027) = December 31, 2030 (Article 31/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) 3 ዓመቱ ከታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2027 = ታኅሳስ 31 ቀን 2030 (አዋጅ 31/3)

Q199  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q199  [Comparative]  COMPARATIVE: Which two funds under Chapter 5 protect startup financing from different angles?
A) Grant Program and income tax exemption
B) Guarantee Fund (debt financing protection) and Fund of Funds (equity financing stimulation)
C) Loss carry-forward and income tax exemption
D) Guarantee Fund and income tax exemption
አማ  ጥ199  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ምዕ 5 ሥሩ ምን ሁለቱ ፈንዶቹ ምን ፋይናንስ?
ሀ) ድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ እና ቀረጥ ቅነሳ
ለ) ዋስትና ፈንዱ (ብድር) እና ፈንዱ-ኦፍ-ፈንዱ (ኢኩዊቲ)
ሐ) ኪሳራ ወደፊት እና ቀረጥ ቅነሳ
መ) ዋስትና ፈንዱ እና ቀረጥ ቅነሳ
EN  Answer: B) Guarantee Fund reduces loan risk for debt financing (Art. 28); Fund of Funds stimulates equity/VC investment (Art. 29)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ዋስትና ፈንዱ ብድር ዋስ (አዋጅ 28); ፈንዱ-ኦፍ-ፈንዱ ቬንቸር ካፒታሉ ቀጥሏ ስታርት አፖቹ (አዋጅ 29)

Q200  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q200  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP CASE STUDY: Co-founder M holds 27% of Startup A and 31% of Startup B. Co-founder N holds 24% of Startup A and 26% of Startup B. Startup A received a grant 8 months ago. Can Startup B apply for a grant?
A) Yes — Startup B is an independent entity
B) No — Co-founder M holds 27% in Startup A (> 25%), barring Startup B; Co-founder N holds 24% in Startup A (< 25%), so N alone would not bar Startup B, but M does
C) No — both co-founders independently bar Startup B
D) Yes — only founders with over 50% are affected
አማ  ጥ200  ብዙ ደረጃ ጥናት፡ ባለቤቶቹ ሀ 27% ስታርት አፕ ሀ እና 31% ስታርት አፕ ለ፤ ባለቤቶቹ ቢ 24% ስታርት አፕ ሀ እና 26% ስታርት አፕ ለ — 8 ወር ቀደም ስታርት አፕ ሀ ድጎማ ያዘ — ስታርት አፕ ለ ሊቀርቡ?
ሀ) አዎ — ሌላ ኩባንያ
ለ) አይቻሏቸዋም — ባለቤቶቹ ሀ 27% ስታርት አፕ ሀ ያዙ → ስታርት አፕ ለ ዕቀባ; ባለቤቶቹ ቢ 24% ብቻ → ቢ ብቻ ዕቀባ ሊሆን አይቻሏቸዋም ነዐ ሀ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
ሐ) ሁለቱ ዕቀባ
መ) ከ50% ብቻ
EN  Answer: B) Co-founder M holds 27% in Startup A → bars Startup B. Co-founder N holds 24% in Startup A → below threshold, so N alone would not bar Startup B. But M's position is sufficient to bar it. (Articles 25/4 and 7/2/b)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ባለቤቶቹ ሀ 27% > 25% → ስታርት አፕ ለ ዕቀባ; ባለቤቶቹ ቢ 24% < 25% → ቢ ብቻ ዕቀባ ሊሆን አይቻሏቸዋም — ሀ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ (አዋጆች 25/4 እና 7/2/ለ)

Q201  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q201  [Multiple Choice]  What must a designated startup do before importing capital goods duty-free?
A) Submit the list of capital goods to the Ministry of Innovation and Technology
B) Submit the list of capital goods to the Ministry of Finance and obtain approval
C) Obtain approval from the Ethiopian Customs Commission directly
D) Apply through the National Startup Designation Committee
አማ  ጥ201  የተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ ከቀረጥ ነፃ ዕቃዎችን ለማስገባት ምን ማድረግ አለበት?
ሀ) ለኢኖቬሽን እና ቴክኖሎጂ ሚኒስቴር የካፒታል ዕቃ ዝርዝር ማቅረብ
ለ) ለፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴር የካፒታል ዕቃ ዝርዝር ማቅረብ እና ፈቃድ ማግኘት
ሐ) ፈቃዱን ቀጥታ ከጉምሩክ ኮሚሽን ማግኘት
መ) ለብሔራዊ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ኮሚቴ ማመልከት
EN  Answer: B) Submit the list of capital goods to the Ministry of Finance and obtain approval (Article 33/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ለፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴር ዝርዝር ማቅረብ እና ፈቃድ ማግኘት ግዴታ ነው (አዋጅ 33/2)

Q202  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q202  [True / False]  True or False: A designated startup can import any goods duty-free without restriction.
አማ  ጥ202  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ የተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ ምንም ዕቃ ያለ ቅድሚያ ፈቃድ ከቀረጥ ነፃ ሊያስገባ ይችላል።
EN  Answer: False — duty-free importation applies to capital goods only, and requires prior Ministry of Finance approval (Article 33/2).
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ቀረጥ-ነፃ ማስገባት ለካፒታል ዕቃዎች ብቻ ሲሆን ቅድሚያ ከፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴር ፈቃድ ያስፈልጋል (አዋጅ 33/2)።

Q203  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q203  [Multiple Choice]  A startup imported machinery duty-free and now wants to sell it to another company. What must it do first?
A) Notify the Ministry of Innovation and Technology within 10 working days
B) Obtain prior approval from the Ministry of Finance before selling or transferring
C) Pay back the waived customs duties, then sell freely
D) Get clearance from the Ethiopian Customs Commission
አማ  ጥ203  አንድ ስታርት አፕ ቀረጥ ነፃ ያስገባውን ማሽን ለሌላ ኩባንያ ለመሸጥ ፈልጓል። ምን ማድረግ አለበት?
ሀ) ለኢኖቬሽን ሚኒስቴር ውሳኔ ካደረጉ ከ10 ቀናት ውስጥ ማሳወቅ
ለ) ቅድሚያ ከፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴር ፈቃድ ማግኘት አለበት
ሐ) ቀረጡ ተከፍሎ ዕቃው ከዚያ ነፃ ይሸጣል
መ) ከጉምሩክ ኮሚሽን ፈቃድ ብቻ ያስፈልጋል
EN  Answer: B) A startup cannot sell, transfer, or dispose of duty-free imported capital goods without prior Ministry of Finance approval (Article 33/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዕቃዎችን ሳይሸጡ ወይም ሳይዛወሩ ቅድሚያ ከፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴር ፈቃድ ማግኘት ያስፈልጋል (አዋጅ 33/3)

Q204  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q204  [True / False]  True or False: The Regulatory Sandbox testing period is unlimited in duration.
አማ  ጥ204  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ የቁጆ ሳጥን ፈተና ጊዜ ያልተወሰነ ነው።
EN  Answer: False — the sandbox operates within a time-bound testing period (Article 2/16 and Article 35).
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — የቁጆ ሳጥን ሙከራ ጊዜ-ተወሰን ነው (አዋጅ 2/16 እና አዋጅ 35)።

Q205  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q205  [Multiple Choice]  Which ministry administers the Regulatory Sandbox under this proclamation?
A) Ministry of Finance
B) Ministry of Trade and Regional Integration
C) Ministry of Innovation and Technology
D) Ministry of Labor and Skills
አማ  ጥ205  በዚህ አዋጅ ሥር የቁጆ ሳጥን (Regulatory Sandbox) የሚያስተዳድረው ሚኒስቴር ማን ነው?
ሀ) የፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴር
ለ) የንግድ እና ክልላዊ ትስስር ሚኒስቴር
ሐ) የኢኖቬሽን እና ቴክኖሎጂ ሚኒስቴር
መ) የሠራተኛ እና ክህሎት ሚኒስቴር
EN  Answer: C) Ministry of Innovation and Technology (Article 35/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) የኢኖቬሽን እና ቴክኖሎጂ ሚኒስቴር (አዋጅ 35/1)


Level 2  —  Easy ⭐
Easy ⭐  |  ቀላል

Q206  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q206  [True / False]  True or False: A startup that exits the Regulatory Sandbox is free from all regulatory obligations.
አማ  ጥ206  ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ለማስገባት ምን ሂደቱ?
ሀ) ቀጥታ ኢቴሚ ፈቃዱ
ለ) ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ዝርዝሩ ሰጥቶ ፈቃዱ
ሐ) ጉምሩክ ብቻ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: False — upon exiting, the startup must comply with all applicable legal requirements (Article 35/5).
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ዝርዝሩ ሰጥቶ ፈቃዱ (አዋጅ 33/2)

Q207  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q207  [Multiple Choice]  A startup borrowed duty-free imported equipment from another company for 2 months. Is this arrangement legally permitted without Ministry of Finance approval?
A) Yes — borrowing is not the same as selling or transferring
B) No — any arrangement that gives another party use of duty-free goods is a form of transfer requiring prior Ministry of Finance approval
C) Yes — temporary arrangements under 6 months are always exempt
D) Only if the borrowing company is also a designated startup
አማ  ጥ207  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዕቃ ሳይሆን ፈቃዱ ቀጥሏ ሊሸጡ ይቻሏቸዋሉ።
EN  Answer: B) Any transfer or disposal of duty-free imported capital goods — including temporary use arrangements — requires prior Ministry of Finance approval (Article 33/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ቅድሚያ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ ሳያሰቡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዕቃዎቹ ሊሸጡ ወይም ሊዛወሩ አይቻሏቸዋም (አዋጅ 33/3)።

Q208  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q208  [Multiple Choice]  What type of goods can a designated startup import duty-free?
A) Raw materials only
B) Capital goods only
C) Both capital goods and raw materials
D) Consumer goods and equipment

ሀ) ምደባ ያዙ ስለሆነ ቀጥሏ
ለ) ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ ሳይጠይቁ ዕቃ ሌሎቹ ፕሮጀክቱ ሊዛወሩ አይቻሏቸዋም
ሐ) ጉምሩክ ቀረጥ ሰጥቶ ሊዛወሩ
መ) ኢቴሚ ቢፈቅዱ ብቻ
EN  Answer: B) Capital goods only (Article 33/1 and 33/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ቅድሚያ ፈቃዱ ሳይጠይቁ ሊዛወሩ አይቻሏቸዋም (አዋጅ 33/3)

Q209  🌐 EN + አማ

ለ) ቴክ ዝውውሩ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ሳያሳውቁ ሊሆን ይቻሏቸዋሉ

D) To allow startups to bypass the designation process

C) Prior Ministry of Finance approval is required for any disposal — including scrapping or auctioning
D) Ethiopian Customs Commission clearance only
አማ  ጥ223  ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ ካለቀ ቀጥሏ ምን?
ሀ) ምርቱ ቀጥሏ ሊሸጡ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
ለ) ደረጃው ፈቃዶቹ ሊያዙ ይገባቸዋሉ
ሐ) ምደባ ካለቀ ምርቱ ሊቆሙ
መ) ቁጆ ሳጥን ታደሶ ሊቀጥሉ
EN  Answer: C) Prior Ministry of Finance approval is required before selling, transferring, or disposing of duty-free imported capital goods under any circumstance (Article 33/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቁጆ ሳጥን ካለቀ ቀጥሏ ምርቱ ሊቀጥሉ ደረጃው ፈቃዶቹ ሊያዙ ይገባቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 37/3)

Q224  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q224  [True / False]  True or False: The Ministry can facilitate access to international intellectual property protection mechanisms for startups.
አማ  ጥ224  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ ስታርት አፕ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ሊያስገቡ ምደባ ሳያሰቡ ቀጥሏ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ዝርዝሩ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ሊሰጡ ብቻ።
EN  Answer: True (Article 36/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ትክክለኛ ምደባ ሁለቱ ቅድሚያ ሁኔታዎቹ ናቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 33/1)።

EN  Q238  [Match]  Match each IP protection measure to its description:
• Anti-piracy measures: _____
• International IP access: _____
• IP advice and facilitation: _____
አማ  ጥ238  ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዕቃ ዝርዝሩ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ሲያቀርቡ ቁጥር ምን?

C) After paying applicable customs duties that were originally waived
D) Following the terms set out in the applicable directive
አማ  ጥ239  ቁጆ ሳጥን ምን ሕጋዊ ውጤቱ ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ላይ ብቻ?
• ቁጆ ሳጥን ሕዝብ ጤና: _____
• ቁጆ ሳጥን ሌሎቹ ዘርፎቹ: _____
EN  Answer: B and D — the disposal requires prior Ministry of Finance approval and is subject to applicable directive terms (Article 33/3 and 33/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሕዝብ ጤና = ሊሰጡ አይቻሏቸዋም (አዋጅ 35/2); ሌሎቹ ዘርፎቹ = ሊሰጡ ይቻሏቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 35/1)

መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ

Q243  🌐 EN + አማ

• Sandbox entry: _____
• Sandbox period: _____
• Sandbox exit: _____

• ሶፍትዌሩ ኮዱ: _____
• ምልክቱ: _____

ሀ) ቀዳሚ ሕጋዊ ፈቃዱ

መ) ምደባ ፈቃዱ ብቻ

D) The machinery is auctioned by the Ethiopian Customs Commission

ለ) ሕጋዊ ዕቀባ — ቀረጥ ሊጣልባቸዋሉ

C) Only foreign startups are permitted to enter the sandbox
D) The sandbox is open to any business, whether designated or not

ለ) ሕጋዊ ዕቀባ — ደረጃው ፈቃዶቹ ያስፈልጋቸዋሉ

Q253  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q253  [Exception Question]  EXCEPTION: Can a startup sell its duty-free imported capital goods to a related-party startup at fair market value?

B) No — any sale, transfer, or disposal of duty-free capital goods requires prior Ministry of Finance approval regardless of the buyer or price
C) Yes — with disclosure to the Ministry of Innovation and Technology
D) Yes — if the Ministry of Finance is notified within 30 days after the transfer
አማ  ጥ253  ልዩ ሁኔታ፡ ቴክ ዝውውሩ ደምቡ ሥሩ ምን ሁኔታዎቹ ሊሟሉ ይቻሏቸዋሉ? (ሁሉ ምረጡ)
ሀ) ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ
ለ) ደምቡ ሥሩ ሁኔታዎቹ
ሐ) ምደባ ያዙ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ

Q254  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q254  [Application Question]  APPLICATION: A startup's technology transfer agreement was not registered with the Ministry. What is the legal consequence?
A) The agreement is still valid but the startup faces a penalty
B) The agreement is unenforceable until registered
C) Article 34/1 requires approval and registration — an unregistered agreement violates the proclamation; the specific consequence depends on the directive
D) No consequence — registration is optional
አማ  ጥ254  ትጋት፡ አዕምሯዊ ንብረቱ ምደባ ሳያዙ ቀደም ሊያዙ ይቻሏቸዋሉ?
ሀ) አይቻሏቸዋም
ለ) አዎ — አዋጅ 36 ምደባ ቀደም ሊያዙ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ ብቻ
መ) ፓቴንቱ ብቻ
EN  Answer: C) Article 34/1 mandates approval and registration; operating under an unregistered agreement is a violation — consequences are set in the directive
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) አዋጅ 36 ምደባ ቀደም ሊያዙ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ

Q255  🌐 EN + አማ

D) The startup is given 90 days to rectify safety issues before enforcement
አማ  ጥ268  ልዩ ሁኔታ፡ ቴክ ዝውውሩ ምን ሕጋዊ ዓይነቶቹ ናቸዋሉ? (ሁሉ ምረጡ)
ሀ) ፓቴንቱ ዝውውሩ
ለ) ሶፍትዌሩ ዝውውሩ
ሐ) ቴክኒካሉ ሰለጠኑ
መ) ምድርት ዝውውሩ
EN  Answer: C) The sandbox operates under regulatory oversight — safety findings during testing would trigger the appropriate regulatory response under applicable laws (Article 35/1 and 35/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ፣ ለ እና ሐ — ምድርት ዝውውሩ ቴክ ዝውውሩ ሳልሆን (አዋጅ 34)

Q269  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q269  [Application Question]  APPLICATION: A startup registers a patent in Ethiopia with Ministry facilitation. A competitor then files a very similar patent application. What must the Ministry do?
A) The Ministry has no role in competing patent disputes
B) The Ministry takes measures to prevent infringement of the startup's IP, including advising on legal remedies
C) The Ministry cancels the competitor's patent automatically
D) The Ministry files a complaint with the Federal Courts on the startup's behalf
አማ  ጥ269  ትጋት፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ ካዳ ቀጥሏ ምን ሌሎቹ ሕጋዊ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ምደባ ቀጥሏ ሁሉ ቀረጥ-ነፃ
ለ) ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ብቻ ፈቃዱ ዝርዝሩ
ሐ) ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዕቃ ቀጥሏ ሁሉ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ፈቃዱ ብቻ
EN  Answer: B) The Ministry takes measures to prevent infringement and provides facilitation — it does not automatically cancel competing applications or litigate on behalf of startups (Article 36/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ብቻ ፈቃዱ ዝርዝሩ (አዋጅ 33/1-2)

C) Ministry of Finance approval is a one-time blanket exemption with no ongoing restrictions

አማ  ጥ270  ቁጆ ሳጥን ምርቱ ፈቃዱ ካዳ ምን ምርቱ ሊፈቀዱ?

ለ) ሕጋዊ ቁጥጥር ሥሩ ጊዜ-ተወሰን ምርቱ ብቻ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሕጋዊ ቁጥጥር ሥሩ ጊዜ-ተወሰን ምርቱ ብቻ ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ሳይካተቱ (አዋጆች 35/1 እና 35/2)

Q271  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q271  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: A startup imported machinery duty-free 3 years ago. It now wants to donate the machinery to a local university. The startup's designation is still valid. What must it do?
A) Donate freely — educational donations are exempt from approval
B) Obtain prior Ministry of Finance approval before donating
C) Notify the Ministry of Innovation and Technology within 10 days after donating
D) Pay customs duties first, then donate freely
አማ  ጥ271  ሁኔታ፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ዝርዝሩ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ሰጡ ፈቃዱ ካዳ ቀጥሏ — ሌሎቹ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ሊያስገቡ — ምን?
ሀ) ቀጥሏ ቀረጥ-ነፃ
ለ) ሌሎቹ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ዝርዝሩ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ሊሰጡ
ሐ) ምደባ ያዙ ስለሆነ ሁሉ ቀረጥ-ነፃ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ፈቃዱ
EN  Answer: B) Any disposal — including donation — requires prior Ministry of Finance approval (Article 33/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሌሎቹ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ዝርዝሩ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ሊሰጡ (አዋጅ 33/2)

C) The Ministry of Finance intervenes to resolve regulatory compliance issues
D) The startup can exit the sandbox voluntarily to avoid the compliance concern
አማ  ጥ274  ትጋት፡ ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ ካዳ ምርቱ ሸጡ ፈቃዱ ሳያልቅ — ምን?

C) The Ministry must cancel all registered agreements upon designation revocation
D) The technology transfer partner must be notified within 10 working days
አማ  ጥ275  ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ ዕቀባ ምን? (ሁሉ ምረጡ)

መ) ቅድሚያ ፈቃዱ ሳያሰቡ ዝውውሩ
