Q276  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q276  [Situational]  SITUATIONAL: A startup imports 3 pieces of manufacturing equipment duty-free. One becomes obsolete after 2 years and it wants to sell it to a local non-designated company. The other two are still in active use. What must the startup do?
A) Sell the obsolete equipment freely — it is no longer needed for the startup's operations
B) Obtain prior Ministry of Finance approval before selling the obsolete equipment
C) Notify the Ministry of Innovation and Technology within 10 days of the sale
D) Pay customs duties on all three pieces of equipment before proceeding
አማ  ጥ276  ሁኔታ ጥናት፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ያዘ ፈቃዱ ካዳ ሌሎቹ ምርቶቹ ሊያስገቡ ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ ሊጠቀሙ — ምን ምን ፍቅዷቸዋሉ?
ሀ) ሁሉ ምደባ ያዙ ስለሆነ
ለ) ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ዝርዝሩ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ; ቁጆ ሳጥን ምርቱ ኢቴሚ ፈቃዱ — ሁለቱ ፈቃዶቹ ሙሉ ነፃ ናቸዋሉ
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ ሁሉ
መ) ምደባ ቀጥሏ ሁሉ
EN  Answer: B) Even for obsolete equipment, prior Ministry of Finance approval is required before sale (Article 33/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ; ቁጆ ሳጥን ምርቱ ኢቴሚ ፈቃዱ (አዋጆች 33/2 እና 35/1)

ሐ) ምደባ ስርዘት ቀረጥ ያስወግዱ
መ) ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ብቻ ይወስኑ

B) Prior approval must be obtained BEFORE any sale or transfer — no time limit from import date, but approval is required whenever the startup intends to dispose of the goods
C) Within 30 days of designation expiry
D) Immediately upon designation expiry

ሀ) ጥር 1 ቀን 2027
ለ) ፈቃዱ ካዳ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ — ጊዜ-ተወሰን ፈቃዱ ዓላማ ካለቀ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ

መ) ምደባ ካለቀ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ
EN  Answer: B) The restriction is event-triggered — prior approval before ANY sale or transfer, not time-limited (Article 33/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቁጆ ሳጥን ጊዜ-ተወሰን ፈቃዱ ዓላማ ካለቀ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 35/4)

A) The sandbox provides the same financial benefits as income tax exemption
B) The sandbox provides regulatory flexibility during testing; income tax exemption provides a direct financial benefit — they serve different purposes
C) Both are available only during the startup designation period
D) The sandbox automatically triggers income tax exemption for participants
አማ  ጥ279  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ እና ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ ምን?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ
ለ) ቁጆ ሳጥን = ኢቴሚ; ካፒታሉ ዕቃ = ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ
ሐ) ሁለቱ ኢቴሚ ፈቃዱ
መ) ሁለቱ ምደባ ብቻ
EN  Answer: B) The sandbox is a regulatory testing mechanism (Art. 35); income tax exemption is a fiscal incentive (Art. 30) — different instruments serving different purposes
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቁጆ ሳጥን = ኢቴሚ (አዋጅ 35/1); ካፒታሉ ዕቃ = ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ (አዋጅ 33/2)

Q280  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q280  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: A startup's technology transfer agreement was approved and registered. The startup later discovers the technology transferred is subject to a third-party patent that was not disclosed. What steps should be taken?
A) The registered agreement automatically protects the startup from third-party IP claims
B) The startup should notify the Ministry; the Ministry may take measures to address the IP infringement issue, and the startup may also pursue remedies under applicable IP and contract law
C) The Ministry cancels the registration automatically upon third-party IP claim
D) The startup must immediately exit any business operations using the technology
አማ  ጥ280  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ሊያስገቡ እና ቁጆ ሳጥን ሊጠቀሙ — ምን ፈቃዶቹ?
ሀ) ምደባ ብቻ
ለ) ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ + ኢቴሚ ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ሁለቱ ፈቃዱ
መ) ኢቴሚ ሁለቱ ፈቃዱ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁለቱ ፈቃዶቹ ሙሉ ናቸዋሉ (አዋጆች 33/2 እና 35/1)

Q281  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q281  [Situational]  SITUATIONAL: A startup that exited the Regulatory Sandbox 6 months ago is still operating without required sector-specific licenses. The relevant regulatory body has filed a complaint. What is the correct legal analysis?
A) Sandbox exit provides a 12-month grace period — no violation yet
B) The startup is in violation — full compliance with all applicable legal requirements is required upon sandbox exit
C) The Ministry of Innovation and Technology must first issue a warning before enforcement
D) Sandbox participants are permanently exempt from sector-specific licensing
አማ  ጥ281  ሁኔታ ጥናት፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ ካዳ — 5 ዓመቱ ካሳ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያ ሸጡ — ምን ቀጥሏ?
ሀ) ቀረጥ-ነፃ ቀጥሏ 5 ዓመቱ
ለ) ፈቃዱ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ሲያሳውቁ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ
ሐ) ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ቅድሚያ ፈቃዱ ሲሰጡ — ቀጥሏ ቀረጥ ሊጣልባቸዋሉ ወይም ሌሎቹ
መ) ሙሉ ምደባ ስርዘት
EN  Answer: B) Sandbox exit requires immediate compliance with all applicable legal requirements — no grace period is provided (Article 35/5)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ቅድሚያ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ ሲሰጡ — ሲፈቀዱ ቀጥሏ ቀረጥ ሊጣልባቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 33/3)

Q282  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q282  [What Happens Next?]  WHAT HAPPENS NEXT: The Ministry discovers that a startup sold its duty-free imported capital goods to a third party without prior approval. What can the Ministry of Finance do?

C) The Ministry of Innovation and Technology revokes the startup's designation immediately
D) The Ministry refers the case to the grievance committee
አማ  ጥ282  ቀጥሎ ምን ይሆናሉ፡ ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ ካዳ ምርቱ ፈቃዱ ካሳ ምርቱ ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ሲሆኑ ምን?
ሀ) ቁጆ ሳጥን ሕዝብ ጤና ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
ለ) ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዕቀባ — ቁጆ ሳጥን ሊሰጡ አይቻሏቸዋም
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
መ) ኢቴሚ ቢፈቅዱ ሕዝብ ጤና ፈቅዷቸዋሉ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዕቀባ — ቁጆ ሳጥን ሊሰጡ አይቻሏቸዋም (አዋጅ 35/2)

አማ  ጥ283  ጊዜ-ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ (ሂደቱ) vs ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ (ሂደቱ) ምን?

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ካፒታሉ ዕቃ = ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ (አዋጅ 33/2); ቁጆ ሳጥን = ኢቴሚ (አዋጅ 35/1)

አማ  ጥ286  ሁኔታ ጥናት፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ያዘ ፈቃዱ ካዳ — ቁጆ ሳጥን ምርቱ ፈቃዱ ካዳ — ሁለቱ ፈቃዶቹ ሕጋዊ?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ምደባ ብቻ
ለ) ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ; ቁጆ ሳጥን ኢቴሚ ፈቃዱ — ሁለቱ ፈቃዶቹ ሙሉ ናቸዋሉ

መ) ኢቴሚ ሁለቱ ፈቃዱ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ፈቃዱ; ቁጆ ሳጥን ኢቴሚ ፈቃዱ (አዋጆች 33/2 እና 35/1)

EN  Q291  [Situational]  SITUATIONAL: A startup imports capital goods duty-free. 4 years later its designation expires naturally (not revoked). It still holds the capital goods. Does the prohibition on selling them without approval persist after designation expiry?

B) Yes — Article 33/3 and 33/4 govern; the disposition of duty-free goods after designation expiry is subject to applicable laws and directive provisions, including any disposal restrictions

አማ  ጥ291  ሁኔታ ጥናት፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ ካዳ ቀጥሏ ቁጆ ሳጥን ምርቱ ፈቃዱ ካዳ ቀጥሏ አዕምሯዊ ንብረቱ ሊያዙ — ምን ፈቃዶቹ?

ለ) ሦስቱ ፈቃዶቹ ሙሉ ናቸዋሉ — ካፒታሉ ዕቃ፣ ቁጆ ሳጥን፣ አዕምሯዊ ንብረቱ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሦስቱ ፈቃዶቹ ሙሉ ናቸዋሉ (አዋጆች 33/2፣ 35/1 እና 36)

አማ  ጥ292  ቀጥሎ ምን ይሆናሉ፡ ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ ካዳ ምርቱ ፈቃዱ ካሳ ፈቃዱ ካለቀ ቀጥሏ ደረጃው ፈቃዶቹ ሳያዙ ቀጥሏ ምን?

መ) ኢቴሚ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: B) Once full compliance is achieved upon sandbox exit, the startup can proceed with commercial operations — no additional sandbox step is required (Article 35/5)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ደረጃው ፈቃዶቹ ሳያዙ ሊሸጡ አይቻሏቸዋም (አዋጅ 37/3)

Q293  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q293  [Timeline]  TIMELINE: A startup's capital goods were imported duty-free on January 1, 2023. The startup's designation is naturally renewed until December 31, 2029. In what year do disposal restrictions on the capital goods end?
A) January 1, 2025 (2 years after import)
B) December 31, 2029 (designation expiry) — subject to applicable directive provisions
C) January 1, 2031 (8 years maximum)
D) The restrictions never end as long as the goods exist
አማ  ጥ293  ጊዜ-ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ vs ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ምን?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዕቀባ
ለ) ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሳልሆን; ቁጆ ሳጥን ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዕቀባ
ሐ) ሁለቱ ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
መ) ሁለቱ ዘርፍ ዕቀባ
EN  Answer: B) The post-designation disposition rules under Article 33/4 govern — applicable directive provisions determine when, if ever, the restrictions end; designation expiry is the key trigger
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሳልሆን; ቁጆ ሳጥን ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዕቀባ (አዋጆች 33 እና 35/2)

Q294  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q294  [Comparative]  COMPARATIVE: How does the Ministry's role regarding the Regulatory Sandbox differ between the entry phase and the exit phase?
A) The Ministry's role is the same throughout — oversight only
B) Entry: Ministry approves entry and sets the testing framework; Exit: Ministry facilitates transition to full regulatory compliance
C) The Ministry has no role at exit — regulatory bodies take over
D) Entry: Ministry of Finance approves; Exit: Ministry of Innovation and Technology facilitates
አማ  ጥ294  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ (ዋጋው) vs ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ (ዋጋው) ምን?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ምደባ ብቻ
ለ) ካፒታሉ ዕቃ = ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ (ዋጋ); ቁጆ ሳጥን = ኢቴሚ (ዋጋ)
ሐ) ሁለቱ ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ
መ) ሁለቱ ኢቴሚ
EN  Answer: B) At entry, the Ministry approves and structures the sandbox (Art. 35/1); at exit, the Ministry facilitates transition to full compliance (Art. 35/5)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ካፒታሉ ዕቃ = ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ (አዋጅ 33/2); ቁጆ ሳጥን = ኢቴሚ (አዋጅ 35/1)

Q295  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q295  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: A startup obtains IP protection for its product with Ministry facilitation, registers a technology transfer agreement, and imports capital goods duty-free. Its designation is then revoked for falsifying documents. What is the combined effect on these three assets?
A) All three are cancelled immediately
B) IP rights follow applicable IP and insolvency laws; technology transfer agreement continues under general contract law; capital goods disposition is governed by applicable laws and directive provisions — none are automatically cancelled
C) IP rights and the technology transfer agreement are cancelled; only capital goods are retained
D) All three are automatically transferred to the government
አማ  ጥ295  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ቴክ ዝውውሩ ምን ዓይነቶቹ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ምንም ዕቀባ
ለ) ደምቡ ሥሩ ሁኔታዎቹ ሳያሟሉ ቴክ ዝውውሩ ሕጋዊ ሳልሆን
ሐ) ምደባ ያዙ ስለሆነ ሁሉ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ ሁሉ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ደምቡ ሥሩ ሁኔታዎቹ ሳያሟሉ ቴክ ዝውውሩ ሕጋዊ ሳልሆን (አዋጅ 34)

Q296  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q296  [Case Study]  CASE STUDY: A startup imports 5 specialized 3D printers duty-free for manufacturing. Two years in, the startup fails commercially and enters liquidation proceedings. Assess the disposition of the 3D printers.
A) The 3D printers are freely sold by the liquidator
B) The liquidator must obtain prior Ministry of Finance approval before selling or disposing of the 3D printers — applicable laws and directive provisions govern their disposition
C) The Ethiopian Customs Commission automatically seizes the 3D printers

አማ  ጥ296  ጥናት፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ ካዳ + ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ ካዳ + ቴክ ዝውውሩ + አዕምሯዊ ንብረቱ — ሁሉ ፈቃዶቹ ምን?
ሀ) ምደባ ብቻ
ለ) ካፒታሉ ዕቃ = ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ; ቁጆ ሳጥን = ኢቴሚ; ቴክ ዝውውሩ = ደምቡ; አዕምሯዊ ንብረቱ = ሕጋዊ ዘዴ
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ሁሉ
መ) ኢቴሚ ሁሉ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ ፈቃዶቹ ሙሉ ናቸዋሉ (አዋጆች 33/2፣ 35/1፣ 34 እና 36)

Q297  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q297  [What Happens Next?]  WHAT HAPPENS NEXT: A startup registers a technology transfer agreement, subsequently receives a grant, imports capital goods duty-free, and enters the sandbox. What is the CORRECT order of Ministry interactions across all four activities?
A) All four are handled by the Ministry of Finance
B) Technology transfer = Ministry of Innovation and Technology (approval/registration); Grant = Ministry of Innovation and Technology (management); Duty-free = Ministry of Finance (approval); Sandbox = Ministry of Innovation and Technology (administration)
C) All four are handled by the Ministry of Innovation and Technology
D) Technology transfer and sandbox = Ministry of Finance; Grant and duty-free = Ministry of Innovation and Technology
አማ  ጥ297  ቀጥሎ ምን ይሆናሉ፡ ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ ፈቃዱ ሳያሰቡ ዕቃ ሸጡ ቀጥሏ ቁጆ ሳጥን ፈቃዱ ካዳ ምርቱ ፈቃዱ ካሳ ፈቃዱ ካለቀ ደረጃው ፈቃዶቹ ሳያዙ ሸጡ — ምን?
ሀ) ሁሉ ሕጋዊ ምደባ ያዙ
ለ) ቀረጥ ሊጣልባቸዋሉ + ደረጃው ፈቃዶቹ ሳያዙ ሕጋዊ ዕቀባ
ሐ) ምደባ ብቻ ስርዘት
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ዳኙ
EN  Answer: B) Correct Ministry assignment: Tech transfer → MoIT (Art. 34/1); Grant → MoIT under Council oversight (Art. 23/2); Duty-free → Ministry of Finance (Art. 33/2); Sandbox → MoIT (Art. 35/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁለቱ ሕጋዊ ዕቀባ ሙሉ ናቸዋሉ (አዋጆች 33/3 እና 37/3)

ለ) ሕጋዊ ቁጥጥር ሥሩ ጊዜ-ተወሰን ሕጋዊ ሽፋን

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሕጋዊ ቁጥጥር ሥሩ ጊዜ-ተወሰን ሕጋዊ ሽፋን (አዋጅ 2/16 እና 35)

Q300  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q300  [Case Study]  CASE STUDY: A startup successfully commercializes a product after exiting the sandbox. A competitor then launches an identical product without a separate IP registration. The startup holds a registered patent. The competitor's product is cheaper. What legal instruments does the startup have available?
A) The startup has no legal recourse unless it has an internationally registered patent
B) The startup can invoke Ministry anti-infringement measures under Article 36/2 AND pursue legal remedies under applicable Ethiopian IP laws; pricing competition is a commercial matter outside IP protection scope
C) The Ministry will cancel the competitor's product registration automatically
D) The startup must renegotiate pricing — IP law does not address price competition
አማ  ጥ300  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ቴክ ዝውውሩ + ቀረጥ-ነፃ ካፒታሉ ዕቃ — ሁለቱ ፈቃዶቹ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዕቀባ
ለ) ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሳልሆን; ቴክ ዝውውሩ ደምቡ ሥሩ ሁኔታዎቹ ዕቀባ
ሐ) ሁለቱ ምደባ ብቻ
መ) ሁለቱ ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: B) Article 36/2 requires Ministry anti-infringement measures; the startup also has remedies under applicable IP law; pricing competition is a commercial matter not covered by IP protection
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቀረጥ-ነፃ ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሳልሆን; ቴክ ዝውውሩ ደምቡ ሥሩ ሁኔታዎቹ ዕቀባ (አዋጆች 33 እና 34)

Q301  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q301  [Multiple Choice]  What does this proclamation allow a designated startup to do regarding the hiring of foreign nationals?
A) Hire an unlimited number of foreign nationals with no restrictions
B) Hire foreign nationals according to a ratio set by a directive
C) Hire foreign nationals only with Council approval
D) Hire only one foreign national per 10 Ethiopian employees
አማ  ጥ301  ይህ አዋጅ የተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ የውጭ ዜጎችን ለቅጥር ምን ያስችለዋል?
ሀ) ያልተወሰነ ቁጥር ያላቸው የውጭ ዜጎችን ያለ ምንም ገደብ ቅጥር
ለ) በመመሪያ የሚወሰን ጥምርታ ሥር የውጭ ዜጎችን ቅጥር
ሐ) የምክር ቤቱ ፈቃድ ካለ ብቻ የውጭ ዜጎችን ቅጥር
መ) ለ10 ኢትዮጵያዊ ሠራተኞች አንድ የውጭ ዜጋ ብቻ
EN  Answer: B) Hire foreign nationals in accordance with a ratio to be set out in a directive (Article 38/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) የውጭ ዜጎችን ቅጥር በመመሪያ ሊወሰን ባለው ጥምርታ ሥር ሊፈፀም ይችላል (አዋጅ 38/1)

Q302  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q302  [True / False]  True or False: A foreign national working in a designated startup must obtain a work permit under the standard Employment of Foreigners Proclamation.
አማ  ጥ302  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ በተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ ውስጥ የሚሠራ የውጭ ዜጋ ሠራተኛ ደረጃው ያለ የሥራ ፈቃድ ሂደት ማለፍ አለበት።
EN  Answer: False — the standard requirement is replaced by a startup-specific permit process under this proclamation (Article 38/2).
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐሰት — ደረጃው ሂደት በዚህ አዋጅ ሥር ባለው ልዩ ፈቃድ ሂደት ተተክቷል (አዋጅ 38/2)።

Q303  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q303  [Multiple Choice]  A foreign expert wants to work at a designated startup. Under this proclamation, does the startup need to follow the standard Employment of Foreigners work permit process?
A) Yes — all foreign national employment must follow the standard process
B) No — designated startups use a startup-specific work permit process that replaces the standard one
C) Only if the foreign expert will be employed for more than 3 months
D) Only if the startup has more than 10 employees
አማ  ጥ303  አንድ የውጭ ዜጋ ባለሙያ በተሰጠ ስታርት አፕ ውስጥ ለመሥራት ፈልጓል። የደረጃ ሥራ ፈቃድ ሂደቱን መከተል ያለበት ወይ?
ሀ) አዎ — ሁሉም የውጭ ዜጋ ቅጥር ደረጃው ሂደቱን ይከተላል
ለ) አይደለም — ተሰጠ ስታርት አፖች ደረጃውን የሚተካ ልዩ ሂደት ይጠቀማሉ
ሐ) ከ3 ወር በላይ ከሠሩ ብቻ
መ) ስታርት አፑ ከ10 ሠራተኞች በላይ ካለው ብቻ
EN  Answer: B) The startup-specific work permit process under this proclamation replaces the standard Employment of Foreigners process for designated startups (Article 38/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ይህ አዋጅ ሥር ያለው ልዩ ሂደት ደረጃው ሂደቱን ይተካዋል (አዋጅ 38/2)

Q317  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q317  [Multiple Choice]  A company wants to produce a product identical to one developed by a designated startup. Beyond the startup's consent, what government approval is required?

አማ  ጥ317  እውነት ወይስ ሐሰት፡ ስታርት አፕ ቪዛ ምደባ ብቻ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ።

D) Protection applies only if the startup has a registered patent
አማ  ጥ320  የውጭ ስታርት አፕ ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ምን?
ሀ) ኢንቨስትመንቱ አዋጅ 1180/2020 ሙሉ
ለ) ኢንቨስትመንቱ አዋጅ 1180/2020 ሳያሠራ
ሐ) 50% ቅነሳ
መ) ቴክኖሎጂ ዘርፍ ብቻ ቅነሳ
EN  Answer: B) No person may replicate, utilize, or appropriate a designated startup's product, service, or process without its prior written consent and Ministry of Innovation and Technology approval (Article 41/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ኢንቨስትመንቱ አዋጅ 1180/2020 ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ለዚህ ስታርት አፕ ሳያሠራ (አዋጅ 40/1)


Level 5  —  Easy ⭐
Easy ⭐  |  ቀላል

Q321  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q321  [True / False]  True or False: A foreign startup that makes a loss on its Ethiopian operations can freely transfer that loss recovery amount abroad.

ሀ) ሁሉ ምርቱ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
ለ) ልዩ ሁኔታ ሳይሆን ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ቅጅ ዕቀባ
ሐ) ምደባ ካዳ ምርቱ ሁሉ ዕቀባ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: True — a foreign startup can freely transfer compensation for losses suffered (Article 40/2/d).
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ልዩ ሁኔታ ሳይሆን ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ቅጅ ዕቀባ (አዋጅ 41/1)

Q328  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q328  [Match]  Match each exception to the replication prohibition with its correct description:
• Valid IP right: _____
• Independent innovation: _____
• Significant improvement: _____
አማ  ጥ328  ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ዓይነቶቹን አዛምድ:
• ትርፍ ዝውውሩ: _____
• ሮያሊቲ ዝውውሩ: _____
• ፈሳሽ ዝውውሩ: _____
EN  Answer: Valid IP right = holder of valid IP right may replicate (Art. 41/2/b); Independent innovation = person independently develops the same product without reference to the startup (Art. 41/2/c); Significant improvement = person makes a significant improvement that constitutes a new startup-eligible product (Art. 41/2/d)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሦስቱ ሁሉ አዋጅ 40/2 ሥሩ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ

አማ  ጥ338  ቅጅ ዕቀባ ልዩ ሁኔታ ዝርዝሩ:
• ሚኒስቴሩ ቅጅ ፈቀደ: _____
• ትክክለኛ IP ያዘ: _____
• ጉልህ ማሻሻሉ: _____
EN  Answer: Hiring ratio = Article 38/1; Competency certificate grace = Article 38/3; Startup-specific work permit = Article 38/2
አማ  መልስ፡ ሦስቱ ሁሉ አዋጅ 41/2 ሥሩ ልዩ ሁኔታ ናቸዋሉ

Q339  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q339  [Multiple Answers]  Which of the following correctly describe income tax treatment of foreign national employees in a designated startup? (Select ALL that apply)
A) Their income is exempt from income tax during the startup's designation period
B) Their income becomes taxable after the designation expires
C) They are also exempt from all other taxes in Ethiopia
D) The exemption applies to all income earned in connection with the startup
አማ  ጥ339  ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ምን ዕቀባ ዘርፎቹ? (ሁሉ ምረጡ)
ሀ) ሁሉ ዘርፎቹ
ለ) ሕዝብ ጤና ሳይካተቱ
ሐ) ቴክኖሎጂ ብቻ
መ) ፊንቴክ ብቻ
EN  Answer: A and B — the exemption is limited to income earned during the designation period; it does not cover all other taxes (Article 30/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ) ሁሉ ዘርፎቹ (አዋጅ 41/1)

Q340  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q340  [Multiple Choice]  What is the minimum capital requirement for a foreign startup under the Ethiopian Startup Proclamation?

C) No minimum capital requirement from Investment Proclamation No. 1180/2020 applies
D) ETB 10,000,000
አማ  ጥ340  ደምቡ ሥሩ ጥምርታ ምን ዓይነቱ?
ሀ) ሁሉ ወሰን ፈቅዷቸዋሉ
ለ) ደምቡ ሥሩ ጥምርታ ብቻ
ሐ) ምደባ ብቻ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ደምቡ ሥሩ ጥምርታ ብቻ (አዋጅ 38/1)

Q341  🌐 EN + አማ

A) It protects designated startups from unauthorized replication of their product
B) It requires Ministry approval for any replication
C) It has no exceptions — no replication is ever permitted
D) It applies only during the startup's designation period
አማ  ጥ341  ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ምን ዓይነቶቹ ዕቀባ? (ሁሉ ምረጡ)
ሀ) ምደባ ሳያዙ ዝውውሩ
ለ) ደምቡ ሥሩ ጥምርታ ሳይሟሉ
ሐ) ብቃቱ ምስክር ወረቀቱ ሳያዙ
መ) ሁሉ ዕቀባ
EN  Answer: A, B, and D — the protection has four explicit exceptions (Article 41)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ) ምደባ ሳያዙ ዝውውሩ (አዋጅ 40/2)

Q342  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q342  [Sequence]  Arrange in CORRECT sequence the exceptions to the replication prohibition as prioritized by the proclamation:
(1) Ministry-approved replication
(2) Replication by holder of valid IP right
(3) Replication through independent development
(4) Replication through significant improvement
አማ  ጥ342  ቅጅ ዕቀባ ቅደም ተከተሉ:
(1) ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ቅጅ
(2) ስታርት አፑ ሪፖርቱ
(3) ምርምር
(4) ሕጋዊ ዘዴ
EN  Answer: Correct order: 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 (Article 41/2/a–d)
አማ  መልስ፡ ቅደም ተከተሉ: 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 (አዋጅ 41/1)

Q343  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q343  [Match]  Match each category of foreign startup earnings to its correct description of what can be transferred abroad:
• Profits and dividends: _____
• Expatriate employee compensation: _____
• IP licensing income: _____
አማ  ጥ343  4-ዓመቱ ብቃቱ ቅነሳ ምን ዘርፎቹ?
• ፈቅዷቸዋሉ: _____
• ዕቀባ: _____
EN  Answer: Profits and dividends = freely transferable abroad; Expatriate employee compensation = freely transferable abroad; IP licensing income (royalties and fees) = freely transferable abroad (Article 40/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ፈቅዷቸዋሉ = ሁሉ ዘርፎቹ ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ሳይካተቱ (አዋጅ 38/3); ዕቀባ = ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ

Q344  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q344  [Multiple Answers]  Which of the following are correctly described regarding a designated startup's protection under Article 41? (Select ALL that apply)

C) Protection applies to the product, service, or process
D) Protection is subject to four explicit exceptions
አማ  ጥ344  ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሲቀዱ ምን ቀዳሚ ዘዴ? (ሁሉ ምረጡ)
ሀ) ቀጥታ ፍርድ ቤቱ
ለ) ሚኒስቴሩ ሪፖርቱ
ሐ) ምደባ ስርዘት
መ) ምርምር

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሚኒስቴሩ ሪፖርቱ (አዋጅ 41/1)

Q348  🌐 EN + አማ

• Standard work permit replaced by: _____
• Hiring ratio determined by: _____
• Competency certificate exception sectors: _____

• ዕቀባ: _____

Q349  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q349  [Multiple Answers]  Which of the following correctly describe how a designated startup is protected against replication? (Select ALL that apply)
A) No person can replicate or reproduce the startup's designated product without approval
B) Holders of valid IP rights are exempt from the prohibition
C) Independent developers of the same product are also prohibited
D) The protection applies to products, services, and processes
አማ  ጥ349  ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ምን ዘዴ ዓይነቶቹ? (ሁሉ ምረጡ)
ሀ) ትርፍ ዝውውሩ
ለ) ካሳ ዝውውሩ
ሐ) ሮያሊቲ ዝውውሩ

ሠ) ፈሳሽ ዝውውሩ

Q350  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q350  [Multiple Choice]  Under this proclamation, how many distinct circumstances allow a person to replicate a designated startup's product without violating the replication prohibition?
A) 2 — Ministry approval or valid IP right only
B) 3 — Ministry approval, valid IP right, and independent development
C) 4 — Ministry approval, valid IP right, independent development, and significant improvement
D) 5 — including an additional export exception
አማ  ጥ350  ቅጅ ዕቀባ ምን ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ሥሩ?
ሀ) ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዘርፎቹ ዕቀባ
ለ) ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዘርፎቹ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ሐ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ ሁሉ

EN  Answer: C) 4 circumstances: Ministry approval, holder of valid IP right, independent development of the same product, and significant improvement constituting a new startup-eligible product (Article 41/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዘርፎቹ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 41/1)

Q351  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q351  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: A company independently creates a product similar to a designated startup's product without knowing about the startup. The startup later claims Article 41 protection. What is the correct legal outcome?
A) The company must stop — the startup has absolute protection
B) The company is exempt — independent development of the same product is an explicit exception under Article 41/2/c
C) The company must obtain Ministry approval retroactively
D) The startup can demand compensation from the company
አማ  ጥ351  ሁኔታ፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ — ቅጅ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
ለ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ሐ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ ሁሉ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: B) Independent development of the same product is an explicit exception to Article 41/1 (Article 41/2/c)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሁሉ ዘርፎቹ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 41/1)

Q352  🌐 EN + አማ

C) The Employment of Foreigners Proclamation governs all foreign national employment, including in startups

ለ) አዎ — ደምቡ ሥሩ ሌሎቹ ሊካተቱ
ሐ) አይደለም — ዝርዝሩ ሙሉ ናቸዋሉ

Q355  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q355  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT about the significant improvement exception under Article 41/2/d?

C) Significant improvements always require Ministry approval even if they meet the definition
D) Only improvements made by ecosystem builders qualify under this exception
አማ  ጥ355  ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሕጋዊ ዘዴ ምን?
ሀ) ቀጥታ ፍርድ ቤቱ
ለ) ሚኒስቴሩ ሪፖርቱ — ምርምር — ሕጋዊ ዘዴ
ሐ) ምደባ ስርዘት
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ዳኙ
EN  Answer: B) The improvement must constitute a significant change that satisfies the definitional requirements of a startup (Article 41/2/d)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሚኒስቴሩ ሪፖርቱ — ምርምር — ሕጋዊ ዘዴ (አዋጅ 41/1)


Level 12  —  Hard ⭐⭐⭐
Hard ⭐⭐⭐  |  ከባድ

Q356  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q356  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: An Ethiopian startup holds designation and is replicating its own product to scale. A competitor applies to the Ministry for approval to replicate the same product. The Ministry refuses. What is the competitor's legal position?
A) The competitor can replicate anyway if it believes refusal was unfair
B) The competitor cannot replicate — Ministry approval is required and was refused
C) The competitor can appeal to the grievance committee
D) The competitor can apply for its own startup designation instead and then replicate freely
አማ  ጥ356  ሁኔታ፡ የውጭ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ሳያዙ ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ፈልጉ — ምን?
ሀ) ምደባ ሳያዙ ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ሊሆን ይቻሏቸዋሉ
ለ) ምደባ ሳያዙ ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ሊሆን አይቻሏቸዋም
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
መ) ፋይናንስ ሚኒስቴሩ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: B) Without Ministry approval and without falling under one of the four exceptions, replication is prohibited (Article 41/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ምደባ ሳያዙ ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ሊሆን አይቻሏቸዋም (አዋጅ 40/2)

Q357  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q357  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT about whether a foreign startup that pays taxes in Ethiopia can also transfer all profits abroad?
A) Foreign startups must pay taxes in Ethiopia and CANNOT transfer profits abroad
B) Foreign startups pay applicable taxes in Ethiopia but are entitled to freely transfer after-tax profits and dividends abroad
C) Foreign startups are exempt from all taxes and can transfer profits freely
D) Foreign startups can transfer profits only if the amount does not exceed their initial investment
አማ  ጥ357  ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሕጋዊ ዘዴ ቅደም ተከተሉ:
(1) ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ቅጅ
(2) ሪፖርቱ ሚኒስቴሩ
(3) ምርምር
(4) ሕጋዊ ዘዴ
EN  Answer: B) Article 40/2/a permits free transfer of profits and dividends — this is after applicable tax obligations are met; income tax exemption applies during designation
አማ  መልስ፡ ቅደም ተከተሉ: 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 (አዋጅ 41/1)

Q358  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q358  [Exception Question]  EXCEPTION: A designated startup holds a registered patent for its product. Another company also holds a registered patent for a very similar product obtained before the startup's designation. Can the other company replicate the startup's product?
A) No — the startup designation takes precedence over all prior patents
B) Yes — the other company holds a valid intellectual property right, which is an explicit exception under Article 41/2/b
C) Only with the startup's written consent
D) Only if both patents are for identical products
አማ  ጥ358  ልዩ ሁኔታ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ምን ዓይነቶቹ ቅጅ?
• ሚኒስቴሩ ቅጅ ፈቀደ: _____
• ትክክለኛ IP ያዘ: _____
• ነፃ ልማቱ: _____
EN  Answer: B) Holding a valid intellectual property right is an explicit exception to the replication prohibition (Article 41/2/b)
አማ  መልስ፡ ሦስቱ ሁሉ አዋጅ 41/2 ሥሩ ልዩ ሁኔታ ናቸዋሉ

Q360  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q360  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT about the relationship between Article 41 and existing intellectual property law?
A) Article 41 replaces all existing IP protection laws for designated startups
B) Article 41 provides an additional layer of protection — holders of valid IP rights are still protected and are exempt from Article 41's prohibition when exercising those rights
C) Article 41 overrides all IP rights held by third parties
D) Article 41 and IP law are completely separate and never interact

ሀ) 10% ወሰን

ሀ) ምደባ ካዳ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ
ለ) ምደባ ካዳ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ — ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ሳይካተቱ
ሐ) ሁሉ ዘርፎቹ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ ብቻ
EN  Answer: B) Article 41/1 protects a designated startup's product, service, or process; ecosystem builder designation is a separate category under this proclamation
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ምደባ ካዳ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ — ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ሳይካተቱ (አዋጅ 38/3)


Level 15  —  Hard ⭐⭐⭐
Hard ⭐⭐⭐  |  ከባድ

Q371  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q371  [Scenario-Based]  SCENARIO: A designated startup claims Article 41 protection against a competitor. The competitor argues it independently developed the product. What is the correct legal test under the proclamation?
A) The competitor must prove it registered its product before the startup's designation
B) The competitor must demonstrate it independently developed the same product without reference to the startup's product
C) The competitor must show it obtained Ministry approval
D) The competitor must prove prior use for at least 1 year before the startup's designation date
አማ  ጥ371  ሁኔታ፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ቅጅ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
ሐ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ ዕቀባ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: B) Independent development without reference to the startup's product is the correct test under Article 41/2/c
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን (አዋጅ 41/2)

Q372  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q372  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT about foreign startup income tax treatment during the designation period?
A) Foreign startups pay income tax at the standard corporate rate
B) Foreign startups are exempt from income tax during the designation period under Article 30/2, just like domestic startups
C) Foreign startups pay a reduced 5% income tax
D) Foreign startups are exempt from income tax permanently once designated
አማ  ጥ372  ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ምን ፈቅዷቸዋሉ?
ሀ) ትርፍ ብቻ
ለ) ትርፉ፣ ካሳዎቹ፣ ሮያሊቲዎቹ፣ ኪሳራዎቹ እና ፈሳሹ
ሐ) ካፒታሉ ብቻ
መ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ ሁሉ
EN  Answer: B) Article 30/2's income tax exemption applies to all designated startups; Article 40 provides additional foreign-startup-specific rights (Article 30/2)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ ዓይነቶቹ (አዋጅ 40/2)

Q373  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q373  [Exception Question]  EXCEPTION: A designated startup's product is being replicated by a university for research. The university claims it is independent development. Which exception might apply?
A) None — research does not qualify as a valid exception
B) Ministry approval exception under Article 41/2/a — the university should obtain Ministry approval
C) Independent development exception under Article 41/2/c if the research genuinely constitutes independent creation without reference to the startup's product
D) The significant improvement exception automatically applies to academic research
አማ  ጥ373  ልዩ ሁኔታ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሦስቱ ዓይነቶቹ:
• ሀ) ሚኒስቴሩ ቅጅ ፈቀደ
• ለ) ትክክለኛ IP ያዘ
• ሐ) ነፃ ልማቱ
• መ) ጉልህ ማሻሻሉ
EN  Answer: C) The independent development exception under Article 41/2/c applies if the university genuinely developed the same product without reference to the startup's product
አማ  መልስ፡ ሁሉ አራቱ ልዩ ሁኔታ ናቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 41/2)

Q374  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q374  [Application Question]  APPLICATION: A company that replicates a designated startup's product with Ministry approval later expands its production beyond what the approval specified. What is the correct legal analysis?
A) The initial approval covers all production scale-ups
B) The Ministry approval covers the scope specified; exceeding that scope may constitute unauthorized replication beyond what was approved
C) No further approval is ever needed once initial Ministry approval is obtained
D) The startup must prove actual harm to trigger enforcement
አማ  ጥ374  ትጋት፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ — ቅጅ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
ሐ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
መ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ
EN  Answer: B) Ministry approval under Article 41/1 covers only what was approved; exceeding the approved scope is unauthorized replication
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን (አዋጅ 41/2)

Q375  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q375  [Which Statement is Correct?]  WHICH STATEMENT IS CORRECT about which sectors are excluded from the 4-year competency certificate grace period for designated startups?
A) All sectors requiring professional licenses are excluded
B) Only public health and national security sectors are explicitly excluded
C) The financial services sector and fintech are also excluded
D) Any sector regulated by a government body is excluded
አማ  ጥ375  4-ዓመቱ ብቃቱ ቅነሳ ምን ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ሥሩ?
ሀ) ቅነሳ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ለ) ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ቅነሳ ዕቀባ ናቸዋሉ
ሐ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ ሁሉ ቅነሳ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: B) Only the public health and national security sectors are explicitly excluded from the grace period — all other sectors, including fintech, benefit from the 4-year window (Article 38/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ቅነሳ ዕቀባ ናቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 38/3)

Q376  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q376  [Situational]  SITUATIONAL: A foreign startup designated in Ethiopia generates profits from operations, receives royalties from licensing its technology internationally, and makes a loss on a failed product line. What are the correct transfer rights for each income type?
A) Only profits can be transferred — royalties and losses are not transferable
B) All three are transferable: profits/dividends (Art. 40/2/a), royalties/fees (Art. 40/2/c), and compensation for losses (Art. 40/2/d)
C) Profits are freely transferable; royalties and losses require Ministry of Finance approval
D) None can be transferred until the designation expires
አማ  ጥ376  ሁኔታ ጥናት፡ የውጭ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ካዳ ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ፈልጉ + 4-ዓመቱ ብቃቱ ቅነሳ + ቅጅ ዕቀባ — ምን ፍቅዶቹ?
ሀ) ምደባ ብቻ
ለ) ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ምደባ ያዘ; 4-ዓመቱ ቅነሳ ምደባ ካዳ; ቅጅ ዕቀባ ምደባ ካዳ
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ሁሉ ፍቅዶቹ
መ) ኢቴሚ ሁሉ ፍቅዶቹ
EN  Answer: B) All three are explicitly listed transfer rights under Article 40/2 (Articles 40/2/a, 40/2/c, and 40/2/d)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሦስቱ ሁሉ ምደባ ካዳ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ (አዋጆች 38/3፣ 40/2 እና 41/1)

Q377  🌐 EN + አማ

C) The Ministry refers the case to the grievance committee

ለ) ሕጋዊ ዕቀባ — ምደባ ሳያዙ ዝውውሩ ሕጋዊ ሳልሆን

Q378  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q378  [Timeline]  TIMELINE: A startup is designated on March 1, 2026, and operates in the fintech sector. By what date must it obtain its required competency certificate?

C) March 1, 2030 (4 years from designation)
D) No deadline — fintech is exempt
አማ  ጥ378  ጊዜ-መስመር፡ ምደባ ጥር 1 ቀን 2026 ካዳ — 4-ዓመቱ ቅነሳ ቀን?

መ) ምደባ ካለቀ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ

Q379  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q379  [Comparative]  COMPARATIVE: How does the replication protection in Article 41 differ from the intellectual property protection under Article 36?
A) They are identical — both protect the startup's product
B) Article 41 provides a designation-based anti-replication protection; Article 36 facilitates access to formal IP rights and prevents infringement — both are complementary layers of protection
C) Article 36 is stronger — Article 41 only applies with Ministry approval
D) Article 41 is stronger — it overrides Article 36 for designated startups
አማ  ጥ379  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ምን ዕቀባ ዓይነቶቹ?
ሀ) ምደባ ሳያዙ
ለ) ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዘርፎቹ
ሐ) ፊንቴክ ዘርፍ
መ) ቴክኖሎጂ ዘርፍ
EN  Answer: B) Article 41 = designation-based protection against unauthorized replication; Article 36 = IP rights facilitation and anti-infringement support — two complementary protective layers
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ) ምደባ ሳያዙ (አዋጅ 40/2)

Q380  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q380  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: A foreign startup designated in Ethiopia wants to hire 3 Ethiopian engineers and 2 foreign data scientists. It also wants to transfer its accumulated profits and IP licensing royalties to its parent company abroad. Walk through the full legal framework for both activities.
A) No restrictions apply — foreign startups operate freely
B) Hiring: foreign nationals hired in a directive-set ratio under startup-specific work permits (Art. 38/1–2); Transfer: profits freely transferable (Art. 40/2/a) and royalties freely transferable (Art. 40/2/c) — both are explicit Article 40/2 entitlements
C) Hiring requires Employment of Foreigners Proclamation compliance; transfers require Ministry of Finance approval
D) All transfers require Council approval; hiring is unrestricted
አማ  ጥ380  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ ጉልህ ማሻሻሉ አሳዩ + ነፃ ልማቱ — ቅጅ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ለ) ሁለቱ ልዩ ሁኔታ ናቸዋሉ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
ሐ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ ዕቀባ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: B) Hiring governed by Article 38/1–2 (ratio + startup permit); Profit transfer = Art. 40/2/a; Royalty transfer = Art. 40/2/c
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁለቱ ልዩ ሁኔታ ናቸዋሉ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን (አዋጆች 41/2/ሐ እና 41/2/ደ)

Q381  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q381  [Situational]  SITUATIONAL: A company obtains Ministry approval to replicate a designated startup's product. The startup's designation is later revoked. Does the Ministry-approved replication continue to be lawful?
A) No — the approval is tied to the designation; when the designation is revoked, the approval also lapses
B) Yes — the Ministry approval was independently granted and remains valid
C) The replication becomes freely permitted for everyone since the startup is no longer designated
D) The replication partner must reapply for Ministry approval after designation revocation
አማ  ጥ381  ሁኔታ ጥናት፡ ቢ.ሲ. ቋሚ ነዋሪ ኢትዮጵያዊ ውጭ ዜጋ ስታርት አፕ ካቋቋሙ — ምደባ ያዙ ቀጥሏ ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ቅናሽ + ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ — ምን?
ሀ) ሁሉ ፍቅዶቹ ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ
ለ) ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ቅናሽ ፍቅዷቸዋሉ; ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ፍቅዷቸዋሉ; ድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ ሳይካተቱ
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ
መ) ኢቴሚ ቢፈቅዱ
EN  Answer: A) The replication protection under Article 41 is tied to the startup's designation status; upon revocation, the approval may no longer have a legal basis
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ቅናሽ + ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ፍቅዷቸዋሉ; ድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ ሳይካተቱ (አዋጆች 40/1፣ 40/2 እና 40/3)

Q382  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q382  [What Happens Next?]  WHAT HAPPENS NEXT: A foreign startup's designation expires naturally. It still has accumulated profits in Ethiopia from its operational period. Can it transfer those profits abroad?
A) No — transfer rights terminate with the designation
B) Yes — profits earned during the designation period are legitimate earnings; Article 40/2/a entitles the foreign startup to transfer profits and dividends
C) Only with Ministry of Finance approval after designation expiry
D) Only the portion earned in the last year of designation can be transferred
አማ  ጥ382  ቀጥሎ ምን ይሆናሉ፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ ስታርት አፑ ሪፖርቱ ሚኒስቴሩ — ምን?
ሀ) ሕጋዊ ዘዴ ቀጥሏ
ለ) ሪፖርቱ ካዳ — ምርምር — ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
ሐ) ምደባ ስርዘት
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ዳኙ
EN  Answer: B) Article 40/2/a entitles foreign startups to transfer profits — profits earned during the designation period remain legitimate transfer entitlements even after expiry
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሪፖርቱ ካዳ — ምርምር — ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን (አዋጅ 41/2)

Q383  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q383  [Timeline]  TIMELINE: A designated startup in the national security sector received its designation on January 1, 2026. By what date must it obtain its competency certificate?
A) January 1, 2030 — the 4-year grace period applies
B) January 1, 2028 — a 2-year grace applies to national security
C) Immediately — the national security sector has no grace period
D) The competency certificate is waived for national security startups
አማ  ጥ383  ጊዜ-ማነጻጸሪያ፡ 4-ዓመቱ ቅነሳ ምን ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ vs ሌሎቹ ዘርፎቹ?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ቅነሳ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ለ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ቅነሳ ዕቀባ; ሌሎቹ ዘርፎቹ ቅነሳ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ሐ) ሁለቱ ቅነሳ ዕቀባ
መ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ቅነሳ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
EN  Answer: C) The national security sector is explicitly excluded from the grace period — the competency certificate is required from the start (or as soon as legally required by applicable law) (Article 38/3)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ቅነሳ ዕቀባ; ሌሎቹ ዘርፎቹ ቅነሳ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 38/3)

Q384  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q384  [Comparative]  COMPARATIVE: How does the income tax treatment of a designated foreign startup compare to a designated Ethiopian startup?
A) Foreign startups pay income tax; Ethiopian startups are exempt
B) Both domestic and foreign designated startups are exempt from income tax during the designation period under Article 30/2
C) Foreign startups are permanently exempt; Ethiopian startups have a time-limited exemption
D) Ethiopian startups are exempt; foreign startups are taxed at a reduced rate
አማ  ጥ384  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ምን ዓይነቶቹ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ምደባ ሳያዙ
ለ) ሕዝብ ጤናና ደህንነቱ ዘርፎቹ
ሐ) ፊንቴክ ዘርፍ
መ) ቴክኖሎጂ ዘርፍ
EN  Answer: B) Article 30/2 applies to all designated startups regardless of nationality — both are exempt during the designation period
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ እና ለ ሳልሆን — ቅጅ ዕቀባ ምደባ ካዳ ሁሉ ዘርፎቹ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 41/1)

Q385  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q385  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: A competitor of a designated startup argues it has a right to replicate under three different exceptions simultaneously: valid IP right (Art. 41/2/b), independent development (Art. 41/2/c), and significant improvement (Art. 41/2/d). Must ALL three be satisfied?
A) Yes — all three must be satisfied simultaneously
B) No — satisfying ANY ONE of the four exceptions is sufficient to be exempt from Article 41/1's prohibition
C) Only the Ministry approval exception (Art. 41/2/a) can override the prohibition — all others are secondary
D) The exceptions must be satisfied in order — the competitor must first try Ministry approval before relying on other exceptions
አማ  ጥ385  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ — ቅጅ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
ሐ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
መ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ
EN  Answer: B) Each exception in Article 41/2/a–d independently exempts the person from the prohibition — satisfying any one is sufficient
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን (አዋጅ 41/2)

Q386  🌐 EN + አማ

A) Nothing — the company can still claim independent development
B) The independent development exception under Article 41/2/c may no longer apply — if the company had access to the startup's confidential documents, the development was not truly independent
C) The company is automatically liable for 100% of the startup's losses

ለ) ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ ምደባ ካዳ; ቅጅ ዕቀባ ምደባ ካዳ — ልዩ ሁኔታ ሲሆን ሳልሆን

Q390  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q390  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: A designated startup holds a product protected by Article 41. Three parties claim exceptions: Party A claims valid IP right, Party B claims Ministry approval, Party C claims significant improvement. Each claim is reviewed. What is the Ministry's standard for each?
A) All three claims must be rejected — only one exception can apply at a time
B) Party A: does it hold a valid IP right covering the product? Party B: was Ministry approval actually granted for replication? Party C: does the improvement constitute a significant change satisfying the definitional requirements of a startup?
C) All three are automatically valid — the startup loses its Article 41 protection
D) Only Party B's claim can be verified — IP rights and improvements are subjective
አማ  ጥ390  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ምን ቅጅ ዕቀባ?

ሐ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ ዕቀባ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ቢፈቅዱ

አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን (አዋጅ 41/2)

Q393  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q393  [Timeline]  TIMELINE: A designated startup's Article 41 protection begins on its designation date. If the startup renews designation 3 times (8-year maximum), and an unauthorized replicator begins replicating in year 6, what is the correct legal analysis?
A) The protection has expired by year 6 — only the first designation period is protected
B) If the startup's designation is still active in year 6 (within the 8-year maximum), the replicator is violating Article 41/1 and subject to enforcement
C) Protection only covers the first 2-year designation period
D) By year 6, replication is freely permitted
አማ  ጥ393  ጊዜ-ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ምደባ ጥር 1 ቀን 2026 ካዳ — 4-ዓመቱ ቅነሳ ቀን + ምደባ ሁለት ዓመቱ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ ቀን?
ሀ) 4-ዓመቱ = ጥር 2030; ምደባ ካለቀ = ታኅሳስ 2027
ለ) 4-ዓመቱ = ጥር 2028; ምደባ ካለቀ = ጥር 2028
ሐ) 4-ዓመቱ = ጥር 2030; ምደባ ካለቀ = ጥር 2028
መ) ሁለቱ ጥር 2028
EN  Answer: B) Article 41 protection applies throughout the active designation period; within the 8-year maximum, the startup's designation and its Article 41 protection remain active
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) 4-ዓመቱ ቅነሳ = ጥር 1 ቀን 2030 (አዋጅ 38/3); ምደባ ካለቀ = ጥር 1 ቀን 2028 (አዋጅ 13/1)

Q394  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q394  [Comparative]  COMPARATIVE: How does Article 41's protection differ from a registered trademark for the same startup product?
A) Article 41 and trademark protection are identical
B) Article 41 = designation-based anti-replication protection; Trademark = an IP right that gives independent exclusivity over the mark — a trademark holder is actually EXEMPT from Article 41/1 under Art. 41/2/b
C) Article 41 is stronger — it overrides all trademarks
D) Trademarks cover the product; Article 41 covers the name only
አማ  ጥ394  ማነጻጸሪያ፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ምን ልዩ ሁኔታ ዘርፍ ምን?
ሀ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ — ሌሎቹ ሳልሆን
ለ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ — ሌሎቹ ሁሉ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ሐ) ሁሉ ዘርፎቹ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ — ልዩ ሁኔታ ሲሆን ሳልሆን
መ) ምደባ ያዘ ስለሆነ ሁሉ
EN  Answer: B) Article 41 provides designation-based protection; a registered trademark is an IP right — making the trademark holder an exception to Article 41/1 under Art. 41/2/b; the two frameworks interact but are distinct
አማ  መልስ፡ ሐ) ሁሉ ዘርፎቹ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ — ልዩ ሁኔታ ሲሆን ሳልሆን (አዋጆች 41/1 እና 41/2)

Q395  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q395  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: A foreign startup receives designation, operates for 3 years, and then transitions to a local entity by selling all shares to Ethiopian nationals, with no foreigner holding more than 50%. Is the entity still a 'foreign startup'?
A) Yes — once foreign, always foreign for purposes of this proclamation
B) No — under Article 2/7, a foreign startup is fully foreign-owned or has a foreigner holding more than half of the capital; after the share sale, neither condition is met — the entity becomes a domestic startup
C) Yes — for 2 years after the transition
D) The Ministry must reclassify the entity formally before the change takes effect
አማ  ጥ395  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ + ምደባ ሳያዙ — ቅጅ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ምደባ ሳያዙ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ለ) ምደባ ሳያዙ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሳልሆን; ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
ሐ) ምደባ ሳያዙ ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ዳኙ
EN  Answer: B) Article 2/7's definition of foreign startup requires full foreign ownership OR foreigner holding more than 50% capital; after the sale, neither is met — the entity is no longer a foreign startup by definition
አማ  መልስ፡ ሀ) ምደባ ሳያዙ ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ (አዋጅ 41/1)


Level 20  —  Expert ⭐⭐⭐⭐
Expert ⭐⭐⭐⭐  |  ጠቢብ

Q396  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q396  [Case Study]  CASE STUDY: A foreign startup operates in Ethiopia: it employs 2 foreign data scientists and 8 Ethiopians, has generated 1,000,000 ETB in profits, 500,000 ETB in IP royalties, and suffered a 200,000 ETB loss. It also holds a product protected by Article 41. Analyze: (1) hiring compliance, (2) transfer rights, and (3) product protection.
A) No restrictions on any of the three areas
B) (1) Hiring: the 2:8 ratio (20% foreign) must comply with the directive; (2) Transfer: profits (Art. 40/2/a), royalties (Art. 40/2/c), and loss compensation (Art. 40/2/d) are all freely transferable; (3) Product: protected under Article 41 during active designation
C) (1) Foreign nationals cannot exceed 1 per 10 Ethiopians; (2) transfers require Ministry of Finance approval; (3) product protection requires separate IP registration
D) All three are governed exclusively by general Ethiopian commercial law
አማ  ጥ396  ጥናት፡ የውጭ ስታርት አፕ ምደባ ካዳ — ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ቅናሽ + ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ + ቅጅ ዕቀባ + 4-ዓመቱ ቅነሳ — ምን ፍቅዶቹ?
ሀ) ምደባ ብቻ
ለ) ሁሉ አራቱ ምደባ ካዳ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ — ድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ ሳይካተቱ
ሐ) ምክር ቤቱ ሁሉ
መ) ኢቴሚ ሁሉ
EN  Answer: B) (1) Ratio must comply with the directive (Art. 38/1); (2) All three amounts are explicitly transferable (Art. 40/2/a, c, d); (3) Article 41 protection applies during designation (Art. 41/1)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ አራቱ ምደባ ካዳ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ (አዋጆች 38/3፣ 40/1፣ 40/2 እና 41/1) — ድጎማ ፕሮግራሙ ሳይካተቱ (አዋጅ 40/3)

Q397  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q397  [What Happens Next?]  WHAT HAPPENS NEXT: A startup protected by Article 41 discovers simultaneous replication by three parties: one with Ministry approval (valid), one holding a registered patent (valid IP right), and one claiming significant improvement. The startup disputes the third party's improvement claim. What is the Ministry's role?
A) All three replications are prohibited — Article 41 has absolute protection
B) Ministry-approved replication = lawful; Patent holder replication = lawful under Art. 41/2/b; Third party's significant improvement claim = Ministry reviews whether it satisfies the definitional requirements of a startup under Art. 41/2/d
C) The Ministry can only review the Ministry-approved replication; the other two are private matters
D) All three must reapply for Ministry approval since the startup disputed them
አማ  ጥ397  ቀጥሎ ምን ይሆናሉ፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ + ምደባ ካዘ — ቅጅ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
ሐ) ምደባ ካዘ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
መ) ምክር ቤቱ ዳኙ
EN  Answer: B) Ministry approval = Art. 41/2/a; Patent = Art. 41/2/b; Improvement = Ministry assesses under Art. 41/2/d's startup definition standard
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን (አዋጅ 41/2)

Q398  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q398  [Timeline & Comparative]  TIMELINE & COMPARATIVE: Compare the total protection duration under Article 41 for a startup that renews to the 8-year maximum vs. a startup whose designation lapses after the first 2 years.
A) Both get 8 years of protection regardless
B) 8-year startup = 8 years of Article 41 protection; 2-year startup = 2 years of protection — protection tracks the active designation period
C) Article 41 protection continues for 3 years after designation lapses in both cases
D) Both get only 2 years since that is the first designation period
አማ  ጥ398  ጊዜ-ሁሉ፡ ምደባ ጥር 1 ቀን 2026 ካዳ — 4-ዓመቱ ቅነሳ + ምደባ ሁለት ዓመቱ ቀቅዋቸዋሉ + ምደባ ስምንቱ ዓመቱ ጣሪያ?
ሀ) ሦስቱ ጥር 2028
ለ) 4-ዓመቱ = ጥር 2030; ሁለቱ = ጥር 2028; ስምንቱ = ጥር 2034
ሐ) 4-ዓመቱ = ጥር 2030; ሁለቱ = ጥር 2027; ስምንቱ = ጥር 2034
መ) ሦስቱ ጥር 2034
EN  Answer: B) Article 41 protection equals the active designation period: 8-year startup = 8 years; 2-year startup = 2 years (Article 41/1 read with Articles 13/1 and 13/4)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) 4-ዓመቱ ቅነሳ = ጥር 1 ቀን 2030; ምደባ ሁለቱ = ጥር 1 ቀን 2028; ምደባ ስምንቱ ጣሪያ = ጥር 1 ቀን 2034 (አዋጆች 38/3፣ 13/1 እና 13/4)

Q399  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q399  [Multi-Step Reasoning]  MULTI-STEP REASONING: A designated domestic startup's product is being replicated under Ministry approval granted 1 year ago. The startup's designation is revoked for falsifying documents. The Ministry-approved replicator wants to continue. What is the full legal analysis?
A) The replicator can continue — its Ministry approval is independent of the startup's designation
B) The replication protection under Article 41 is tied to the startup's designation; upon revocation, the foundational basis for the Article 41 prohibition (and thus the Ministry-granted approval to replicate) may lapse — the replicator should seek Ministry guidance
C) The replicator automatically receives the startup's designation
D) The replicator can continue for 1 year after the revocation date
አማ  ጥ399  ማነጻጸሪያ-ሁሉ፡ ምን ሁሉ ማበረታቻዎቹ ሀገር ውስጥ ስታርት አፕ vs የውጭ ስታርት አፕ?
ሀ) ሁለቱ ተመሳሳይ
ለ) ሀገር ውስጥ = ሁሉ ጨምሮ ድጎማ; የውጭ = ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ቅናሽ + ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ + ቅጅ ዕቀባ — ድጎማ ሳይካተቱ
ሐ) የውጭ = ሁሉ ጨምሮ ድጎማ
መ) ሀገር ውስጥ = ቅጅ ዕቀባ ሳይካተቱ
EN  Answer: B) Article 41's prohibition exists because the startup is designated; revocation removes the designation basis; Ministry-approved replication under Article 41/2/a may no longer be needed or valid — Ministry guidance is appropriate
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሀገር ውስጥ ሁሉ ማበረታቻዎቹ; የውጭ ዝቅተኛ ካፒታሉ ቅናሽ + ሕጋዊ ዝውውሩ + ቅጅ ዕቀባ — ድጎማ ሳይካተቱ (አዋጆች 40/1፣ 40/2፣ 40/3 እና 41)

Q400  🌐 EN + አማ
EN  Q400  [Case Study]  CASE STUDY FINAL: A foreign startup has operated for 6 years under Ethiopian designation. It now plans to: (1) transfer all assets to a new Ethiopian entity it will own 60%; (2) transfer all profits, royalties, and compensation for losses abroad before the transfer; (3) continue operating the new entity without a foreign startup designation. Analyze the legal implications.
A) All three steps are fully permitted with no legal complications
B) (1) With a 60% foreign ownership stake, the new entity remains a 'foreign startup' under Article 2/7; (2) All three transfers are explicitly permitted under Article 40/2 before transition; (3) The new entity must apply for designation separately — Article 41 and other protections require active designation
C) The 60% ownership automatically disqualifies the new entity from any startup designation
D) Transfers abroad are prohibited after the decision to restructure is made
አማ  ጥ400  ብዙ ደረጃ ምክንያት-ሁሉ፡ ሌሎቹ ኩባንያዎቹ ምርቱ ሰበቁ — ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ + ምደባ ካዘ + ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ — ቅጅ ዕቀባ?
ሀ) ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን
ሐ) ሕዝብ ጤና ዘርፍ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
መ) ምደባ ካዘ ዕቀባ ሸፍኗቸዋሉ
EN  Answer: B) (1) 60% foreign ownership means it meets the Art. 2/7 foreign startup definition; (2) Art. 40/2/a, c, d permit those transfers; (3) Designation does not automatically transfer — the new entity must apply (Article 2/7 and Article 7)
አማ  መልስ፡ ለ) ሁሉ ልዩ ሁኔታ ፈፀሙ — ዕቀባ ሳልሆን (አዋጅ 41/2)
